=== FILE: ./lib/handlers/missionaryHandler.js ===

import { runSql } from '../db.js';

export async function handleMissionaryAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_missionaries") {
    const rows = await runSql("SELECT * FROM missionaries ORDER BY is_prelisted DESC, name ASC");
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, missionaries: rows || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "update_missionary_points") {
    const email = (bodyData.email || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    const delta = Number(bodyData.delta) || 0;
    if (!email) return { status: 400, json: { ok: false, error: "Missing email address" } };

    await runSql("UPDATE missionaries SET points = MAX(0, points + ?) WHERE LOWER(email) = ?", [delta, email]);
    await runSql("INSERT INTO system_logs (level, message) VALUES ('INFO', ?)", [`Updated points for ${email} by delta ${delta}`]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "delete_missionary") {
    const email = (bodyData.email || req.query?.email || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!email) return { status: 400, json: { ok: false, error: "Missing missionary email address" } };

    const missionary = (await runSql("SELECT psid FROM missionaries WHERE LOWER(email) = ?", [email]))[0];
    if (missionary?.psid) {
      await runSql("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE psid = ?", [missionary.psid]);
    }
    await runSql("DELETE FROM missionaries WHERE LOWER(email) = ?", [email]);
    await runSql("INSERT INTO system_logs (level, message) VALUES ('WARN', ?)", [`Removed missionary ${email} from roster`]);

    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, message: `Successfully deleted missionary ${email}` } };
  }

  if (action === "push_missionaries") {
    if (req.method === "GET") {
      const logs = await runSql("SELECT email, name, last_name, cohort, batch_month FROM missionaries WHERE is_prelisted = 1 ORDER BY ROWID DESC LIMIT 50");
      return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, history: logs || [] } };
    }

    if (req.method === "POST") {
      const entries = bodyData.entries || [];
      let added = 0;

      for (const item of entries) {
        const email = (item.email || "").toLowerCase().trim();
        const titleName = (item.title_name || item.name || "").trim();
        const firstName = (item.first_name || "").trim();
        const batchMonth = (item.batch || "August 2026").trim();

        if (!email || !titleName) continue;

        let cohort = "elder";
        let maxMonths = 24;
        if (/^sister\b/i.test(titleName)) {
          cohort = "sister";
          maxMonths = 18;
        }

        const lastName = titleName.replace(/^(elder|sister)\s+/i, "").trim();
        const fullName = `${titleName} ${firstName}`.trim();

        const existing = (await runSql("SELECT email FROM missionaries WHERE LOWER(email) = ?", [email]))[0];
        if (existing) {
          await runSql(
            "UPDATE missionaries SET name = ?, last_name = ?, first_name = ?, full_name = ?, cohort = ?, batch_month = ?, max_months = ?, is_prelisted = 1 WHERE LOWER(email) = ?",
            [titleName, lastName, firstName, fullName, cohort, batchMonth, maxMonths, email]
          );
          added++;
          continue;
        }

        const chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ";
        const nums = "23456789";
        let refCode = "";
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          refCode += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
          refCode += nums.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * nums.length));
        }

        await runSql(
          "INSERT INTO missionaries (email, name, last_name, first_name, full_name, cohort, batch_month, referral_code, max_months, points, status, is_prelisted) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 'active', 1)",
          [email, titleName, lastName, firstName, fullName, cohort, batchMonth, refCode, maxMonths]
        );
        added++;
      }

      await runSql("INSERT INTO system_logs (level, message) VALUES ('INFO', ?)", [`Successfully bulk pushed ${added} missionaries`]);
      return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, added } };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

import { runSql } from '../db.js';

export async function handlePromoAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_promo_codes") {
    const rows = await runSql("SELECT * FROM promo_codes ORDER BY created_at DESC");
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, promo_codes: rows || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "save_promo_code") {
    const { code, points, max_users } = bodyData;
    const cleanCode = (code || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const pts = Number(points) || 1;
    const maxUsr = Number(max_users) || 30;

    if (!cleanCode) return { status: 400, json: { ok: false, error: "Missing promo code" } };

    await runSql(`
      INSERT INTO promo_codes (code, points, max_users, claimed_count)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)
      ON CONFLICT(code) DO UPDATE SET points = excluded.points, max_users = excluded.max_users
    `, [cleanCode, pts, maxUsr]);

    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "delete_promo_code") {
    const { code } = bodyData;
    await runSql("DELETE FROM promo_codes WHERE code = ?", [code]);
    await runSql("DELETE FROM promo_redemptions WHERE code = ?", [code]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  return null;
}

=== FILE: ./lib/handlers/emailHandler.js ===

import { runSql } from '../db.js';
import { sendDripEmail, sendOTPEmail, sendReceiptEmail, sendThankYouEmail } from '../mailer.js';

export async function handleEmailAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "test_email") {
    const rawEmails = (bodyData.email || req.query?.email || "").trim();
    const templateType = bodyData.template_type || req.query?.template_type || "drip";
    const month = Number(bodyData.month || req.query?.month) || 1;

    if (!rawEmails) return { status: 400, json: { ok: false, error: "Missing email address(es)" } };

    const targets = rawEmails.split(",").map(e => e.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    let successCount = 0;
    const results = [];

    for (const target of targets) {
      let dispatchResult;
      if (templateType === "otp") {
        dispatchResult = await sendOTPEmail(target, "891402");
      } else if (templateType === "receipt") {
        dispatchResult = await sendReceiptEmail(target, {
          name: "Elder / Sister Diagnostic",
          order_id: "TCRP-" + Date.now().toString().slice(-4),
          item: "Wooden Missionary Nametag",
          points_cost: 6
        });
      } else if (templateType === "thankyou") {
        dispatchResult = await sendThankYouEmail(target, {
          name: "Elder / Sister Diagnostic",
          order_id: "TCRP-" + Date.now().toString().slice(-4),
          item: "Wooden Missionary Nametag"
        });
      } else {
        dispatchResult = await sendDripEmail(target, month, "Elder / Sister");
      }

      results.push({ email: target, ...dispatchResult });
      if (dispatchResult?.ok) successCount++;
    }

    await runSql("INSERT INTO system_logs (level, message) VALUES ('INFO', ?)", [`Executed test email dispatch (${templateType}) to ${targets.join(', ')}`]);

    if (successCount > 0) {
      return {
        status: 200,
        json: {
          ok: true,
          totalSent: successCount,
          template: templateType,
          month: templateType === "drip" ? month : undefined,
          recipients: targets,
          details: results
        }
      };
    }

    return {
      status: 500,
      json: {
        ok: false,
        error: "Brevo REST API rejected dispatch.",
        details: results
      }
    };
  }

  return null;
}

import { runSql } from '../db.js';
import { sendThankYouEmail, sendOrderStatusEmail } from '../mailer.js';

export async function handleCatalogAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_products") {
    const typeFilter = req.query?.type || bodyData.type;
    let query = "SELECT id, name, CAST(price AS INTEGER) as price, image_url, type FROM product_catalog";
    let params = [];
    if (typeFilter) {
      query += " WHERE type = ? ORDER BY price ASC";
      params.push(typeFilter);
    } else {
      query += " ORDER BY type ASC, price ASC";
    }
    const products = await runSql(query, params);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, products: products || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "sync_catalog" || action === "save_products") {
    const products = bodyData.products || req.body?.products || [];
    const catalogType = bodyData.type || req.body?.type || "reward";

    await runSql("DELETE FROM product_catalog WHERE type = ?", [catalogType]);

    for (const item of products) {
      if (!item.name) continue;
      const cost = parseFloat(item.price) || 0;
      const img = item.image_url || "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";

      await runSql(
        "INSERT INTO product_catalog (name, price, image_url, type) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET price = excluded.price, image_url = excluded.image_url, type = excluded.type",
        [item.name.trim(), cost, img, catalogType]
      );
    }
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, message: `${catalogType} catalog synchronized successfully` } };
  }

  if (action === "get_orders") {
    const orders = await runSql("SELECT * FROM orders ORDER BY created_at DESC");
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, orders: orders || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "update_order_status") {
    const { order_id, status } = bodyData;
    const cleanStatus = (status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase();
    await runSql("UPDATE orders SET status = ? WHERE order_id = ?", [cleanStatus, order_id]);
    
    const order = (await runSql("SELECT * FROM orders WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id]))[0];
    if (order && order.email) {
      if (cleanStatus === 'PAID' || cleanStatus === 'COMPLETED') {
        await sendThankYouEmail(order.email, { name: order.name, order_id: order.order_id, item: order.item });
      } else {
        await sendOrderStatusEmail(order.email, { name: order.name, order_id: order.order_id, item: order.item }, cleanStatus);
      }
    }
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "update_order") {
    const { order_id, items_json, status } = bodyData;
    const cleanStatus = (status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase();
    const itemsList = Array.isArray(items_json) ? items_json.map(i => `${i.qty}x ${i.name}`).join(', ') : '';
    
    await runSql(`
      UPDATE orders 
      SET items_json = ?, item = ?, status = ?
      WHERE order_id = ?
    `, [JSON.stringify(items_json || []), itemsList, cleanStatus, order_id]);

    const order = (await runSql("SELECT * FROM orders WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id]))[0];
    if (order && order.email) {
      if (cleanStatus === 'PAID' || cleanStatus === 'COMPLETED') {
        await sendThankYouEmail(order.email, { name: order.name, order_id: order.order_id, item: order.item });
      } else {
        await sendOrderStatusEmail(order.email, { name: order.name, order_id: order.order_id, item: order.item }, cleanStatus);
      }
    }
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "delete_order") {
    const { order_id } = bodyData;
    await runSql("DELETE FROM orders WHERE order_id = ?", [order_id]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  return null;
}

import { runSql } from '../db.js';

export async function handleDripAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_drips") {
    const drips = await runSql("SELECT * FROM drip_messages ORDER BY month ASC");
    const configRows = await runSql("SELECT key, value FROM system_config WHERE key LIKE 'drip_%_highlight_2'");
    const highlight2Map = {};
    (configRows || []).forEach(r => {
      try { highlight2Map[r.key] = JSON.parse(r.value); } catch(_) {}
    });

    const mergedDrips = (drips || []).map(d => {
      const extra = highlight2Map[`drip_${d.month}_highlight_2`];
      return {
        ...d,
        highlight_label_2: extra?.label || "",
        highlight_img_2: extra?.img || ""
      };
    });

    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, drips: mergedDrips } };
  }

  if (action === "save_drip") {
    const { month, subject, theme, scripture, message, highlight_img, highlight_label, highlight_img_2, highlight_label_2, custom_html } = bodyData;
    await runSql(`
      INSERT INTO drip_messages (month, subject, theme, scripture, message, highlight_img, highlight_label, custom_html)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(month) DO UPDATE SET
        subject = excluded.subject,
        theme = excluded.theme,
        scripture = excluded.scripture,
        message = excluded.message,
        highlight_img = excluded.highlight_img,
        highlight_label = excluded.highlight_label,
        custom_html = excluded.custom_html
    `, [
      Number(month) || 1,
      subject || "",
      theme || "",
      scripture || "",
      message || "",
      highlight_img || "",
      highlight_label || "",
      custom_html || ""
    ]);

    if (highlight_label_2) {
      const val = JSON.stringify({ label: highlight_label_2, img: highlight_img_2 || "" });
      await runSql("INSERT INTO system_config (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", [`drip_${month}_highlight_2`, val]);
    } else {
      await runSql("DELETE FROM system_config WHERE key = ?", [`drip_${month}_highlight_2`]);
    }

    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  return null;
}

import { runSql } from '../db.js';
import { sendThankYouEmail, sendOrderStatusEmail } from '../mailer.js';

export async function handleInvoiceAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_invoices") {
    const invoices = await runSql("SELECT * FROM cash_invoices ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50");
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, invoices: invoices || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "update_invoice_status") {
    const { invoice_id, status } = bodyData;
    const cleanStatus = (status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase();
    await runSql("UPDATE cash_invoices SET status = ? WHERE invoice_id = ?", [cleanStatus, invoice_id]);
    
    const inv = (await runSql("SELECT * FROM cash_invoices WHERE invoice_id = ?", [invoice_id]))[0];
    if (inv && inv.email) {
      let itemsList = "Custom Order";
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(inv.items_json);
        itemsList = parsed.map(i => `${i.qty}x ${i.name}`).join(', ');
      } catch(_) {}

      if (cleanStatus === 'PAID' || cleanStatus === 'COMPLETED') {
        await sendThankYouEmail(inv.email, { name: inv.name, order_id: inv.invoice_id, item: itemsList });
      } else {
        await sendOrderStatusEmail(inv.email, { name: inv.name, order_id: inv.invoice_id, item: itemsList }, cleanStatus);
      }
    }
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "update_invoice") {
    const { invoice_id, items_json, subtotal, discount_type, discount_val, discount_amount, total_amount, status } = bodyData;
    const cleanStatus = (status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase();
    await runSql(`
      UPDATE cash_invoices 
      SET items_json = ?, subtotal = ?, discount_type = ?, discount_val = ?, discount_amount = ?, total_amount = ?, status = ?
      WHERE invoice_id = ?
    `, [JSON.stringify(items_json || []), subtotal, discount_type, discount_val, discount_amount, total_amount, cleanStatus, invoice_id]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "delete_invoice") {
    const { invoice_id } = bodyData;
    await runSql("DELETE FROM cash_invoices WHERE invoice_id = ?", [invoice_id]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "create_invoice") {
    const { invoice_id, email, name, items_json, subtotal, discount_type, discount_val, discount_amount, total_amount } = bodyData;
    await runSql(`
      INSERT INTO cash_invoices (invoice_id, email, name, items_json, subtotal, discount_type, discount_val, discount_amount, total_amount, status, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
    `, [invoice_id, email, name, JSON.stringify(items_json || []), subtotal, discount_type, discount_val, discount_amount, total_amount]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  return null;
}

=== FILE: ./lib/handlers/cdnHandler.js ===

import { runSql } from '../db.js';

export async function handleCdnAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "get_cdn_config") {
    const rows = await runSql("SELECT key, value FROM system_config WHERE key LIKE 'cdn_%'");
    const config = {};
    (rows || []).forEach(r => { config[r.key] = r.value; });
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, config } };
  }

  if (action === "save_cdn_config") {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(bodyData)) {
      if (k.startsWith('cdn_')) {
        await runSql("INSERT INTO system_config (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", [k, String(v || '')]);
      }
    }
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  if (action === "list" || action === "cdn_list" || action === "cdn_gallery" || action === "get_cdn_gallery") {
    const rows = await runSql("SELECT * FROM cdn_gallery ORDER BY id DESC");
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, gallery: rows || [], items: rows || [] } };
  }

  if (action === "upload" || action === "cdn_upload") {
    const { filename, targetSize, originalKb, compressedKb, base64Data } = bodyData;
    
    let owner = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_OWNER || 'AllensCreations';
    let repo = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_REPO || 'Gallery';
    let branch = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_BRANCH || 'main';
    let uploadPath = process.env.CDN_UPLOAD_PATH || 'assets/rewards';
    let token = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_TOKEN;

    try {
      const configRows = await runSql("SELECT key, value FROM system_config WHERE key LIKE 'cdn_%'");
      const dbConfig = {};
      (configRows || []).forEach(r => { dbConfig[r.key] = r.value; });
      owner = dbConfig.cdn_github_owner || owner;
      repo = dbConfig.cdn_github_repo || repo;
      branch = dbConfig.cdn_github_branch || branch;
      uploadPath = dbConfig.cdn_upload_path || uploadPath;
      token = token || dbConfig.cdn_github_token;
    } catch (_) {}

    const cleanFilename = (filename || `image_${Date.now()}.webp`).trim();
    const filePath = `${uploadPath}/${cleanFilename}`.replace(/^\/+\//, '');
    const directUrl = `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/${owner}/${repo}@${branch}/${filePath}`;

    if (base64Data && token) {
      const githubApiUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${owner}/${repo}/contents/${filePath}`;
      const base64Content = base64Data.includes('base64,') ? base64Data.split('base64,')[1] : base64Data;

      let sha = null;
      try {
        const checkRes = await fetch(`${githubApiUrl}?ref=${branch}`, {
          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'User-Agent': 'TCRP-CDN-Uploader', 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json' }
        });
        if (checkRes.ok) {
          const checkData = await checkRes.json();
          sha = checkData.sha;
        }
      } catch (_) {}

      const commitPayload = {
        message: `Upload CDN asset ${cleanFilename} via TCRP Gallery`,
        content: base64Content,
        branch: branch
      };
      if (sha) commitPayload.sha = sha;

      const commitRes = await fetch(githubApiUrl, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
          'User-Agent': 'TCRP-CDN-Uploader',
          'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(commitPayload)
      });

      if (!commitRes.ok) {
        const errJson = await commitRes.json().catch(() => ({}));
        return { status: 500, json: { ok: false, error: `GitHub Commit Failed: ${errJson.message || commitRes.statusText}` } };
      }
    }

    await runSql(`
      INSERT INTO cdn_gallery (filename, direct_url, size_label, original_kb, compressed_kb)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `, [cleanFilename, directUrl, targetSize || 'square_600', Number(originalKb) || 0, Number(compressedKb) || 0]);

    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true, direct_url: directUrl, item: { filename: cleanFilename, direct_url: directUrl, size_label: targetSize } } };
  }

  if (action === "delete" || action === "cdn_delete") {
    const id = Number(bodyData.id || req.query?.id);
    await runSql("DELETE FROM cdn_gallery WHERE id = ?", [id]);
    return { status: 200, json: { ok: true } };
  }

  return null;
}

=== FILE: ./lib/handlers/botApiHandler.js ===

export async function handleBotApiAction(action, req, bodyData) {
  if (action === "setup_messenger_profile") {
    const pageToken = (process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || "").trim();
    if (!pageToken) return { status: 400, json: { ok: false, error: "Missing PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN" } };

    const profilePayload = {
      get_started: { payload: "GET_STARTED" },
      persistent_menu: [
        {
          locale: "default",
          composer_input_disabled: false,
          call_to_actions: [
            { type: "postback", title: "📊 My Dashboard", payload: "ACTION_DASHBOARD" },
            { type: "postback", title: "🎁 Rewards Catalog", payload: "DISCOVER_PAYLOAD" },
            { type: "postback", title: "📖 FAQs & Help", payload: "FAQS_PAYLOAD" },
            { type: "postback", title: "🎟️ Redeem Promo Code", payload: "PROMO_INFO" }
          ]
        }
      ]
    };

    try {
      const fbRes = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me/messenger_profile?access_token=${pageToken}`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(profilePayload)
      });
      const fbData = await fbRes.json();
      return { status: 200, json: { ok: !fbData.error, fb_response: fbData } };
    } catch (err) {
      return { status: 500, json: { ok: false, error: err.message } };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

<script>
let currentMonth = 1;
let liveDripMap = {};
let masterTemplateCode = "";
let currentTargetInputId = null;
let currentTargetThumbId = null;

const TEMPLE_IMG_URL = "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1IkagW3wWhIhfaG01mBL4wNF-1j2lP6YG";

function getCalendarMonthLabel(monthIndex) {
  const calendarNames = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
  const idx = (Number(monthIndex) - 1) % 12;
  return calendarNames[idx];
}

function switchTab(tabId, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("active"));
  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add("active");
  btn.classList.add("active");
}

function initSelector() {
  const sel = document.getElementById("month-selector");
  sel.innerHTML = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
    const opt = document.createElement("option");
    opt.value = i;
    opt.textContent = `${getCalendarMonthLabel(i)} (Month ${i})`;
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  }
}

// Open Gallery Modal Picker
async function openGalleryModal(inputId, thumbId) {
  currentTargetInputId = inputId;
  currentTargetThumbId = thumbId;
  document.getElementById("gallery-picker-modal").style.display = "flex";
  
  let items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', null);
  if (!items) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=cdn_list');
      const d = await res.json();
      items = d.gallery || d.items || [];
      LocalStore.set('tcrp_cdn_gallery', items);
    } catch (_) { items = []; }
  }
  renderModalGrid(items);
}

function closeGalleryModal() {
  document.getElementById("gallery-picker-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function renderModalGrid(items) {
  const grid = document.getElementById("modal-grid-container");
  if (!items || !items.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No images in Gallery. Upload images in CDN Gallery first!</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = items.map(it => `
    <div class="modal-img-card" onclick="selectImage('${it.direct_url}')" title="${it.filename}">
      <img src="${it.direct_url}" alt="${it.filename}" loading="lazy">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function filterModalGrid(q) {
  const clean = (q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', []);
  const filtered = items.filter(i => (i.filename || '').toLowerCase().includes(clean));
  renderModalGrid(filtered);
}

function selectImage(url) {
  if (currentTargetInputId) {
    document.getElementById(currentTargetInputId).value = url;
  }
  if (currentTargetThumbId) {
    document.getElementById(currentTargetThumbId).src = url;
  }
  closeGalleryModal();
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Image selected from gallery!");
}

function revertImage(inputId, thumbId, defaultUrl) {
  document.getElementById(inputId).value = defaultUrl;
  document.getElementById(thumbId).src = defaultUrl;
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("↺ Reverted to default image.");
}

async function fetchFromTurso() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_drips");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && Array.isArray(data.drips)) {
      data.drips.forEach(d => {
        liveDripMap[Number(d.month)] = {
          month: Number(d.month),
          subject: d.subject || `Monthly Encouragement (${getCalendarMonthLabel(d.month)}) • Timeless Creations`,
          theme: d.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
          scripture: d.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
          message: d.message || `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others during ${getCalendarMonthLabel(d.month)}.`,
          highlight_img: d.highlight_img || "",
          highlight_label: d.highlight_label || "",
          highlight_img_2: d.highlight_img_2 || "",
          highlight_label_2: d.highlight_label_2 || "",
          custom_html: d.custom_html || ""
        };
      });
    }

    masterTemplateCode = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark">
<style>
  body { font-family: 'Garamond', 'Georgia', serif; background-color: #f9f7f2; color: #1a1a1a; margin: 0; padding: 20px 0; }
  .email-container { max-width: 450px; background: #ffffff; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e0d6bc; box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.03); }
  .brand-header { padding: 35px 20px 15px; text-align: center; }
  .logo-text { font-size: 24px; letter-spacing: 5px; text-transform: uppercase; font-weight: 300; margin: 0; color: #1a1a1a; }
  .temple-img { width: 100%; height: auto; min-height: 160px; object-fit: cover; display: block; border: 0; }
  .main-content { padding: 22px 18px; text-align: center; line-height: 1.6; }
  .quote-container { margin: 18px 0; padding: 18px; background-color: #fdfbf8; border-left: 1px solid #d4c197; border-right: 1px solid #d4c197; }

function onSelectMonth(m) {
  currentMonth = parseInt(m, 10);
  const label = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);
  document.getElementById("unsaved-month-label").textContent = `${label} (Month ${currentMonth})`;

  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {
    month: currentMonth,
    subject: `Monthly Encouragement (${label}) • Timeless Creations`,
    theme: "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
    scripture: "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
    message: `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others during ${label}.`,
    highlight_img: "",
    highlight_label: "",
    highlight_img_2: "",
    highlight_label_2: "",
    custom_html: ""
  };

  document.getElementById("inp-subject").value = data.subject || `Monthly Encouragement (${label}) • Timeless Creations`;
  document.getElementById("inp-theme").value = data.theme || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-msg").value = data.message || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-quote").value = data.scripture || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value = data.custom_html || "";

  if ((data.highlight_label && data.highlight_label.trim() !== "") || (data.highlight_label_2 && data.highlight_label_2.trim() !== "")) {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = true;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = data.highlight_label || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value = data.highlight_img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE";
    document.getElementById("thumb-hl-1").src = data.highlight_img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = data.highlight_label_2 || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value = data.highlight_img_2 || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ";
    document.getElementById("thumb-hl-2").src = data.highlight_img_2 || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = false;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = "";
  }

  updateStatusBadge(data.custom_html);
  renderPreview();
}

function updateStatusBadge(hasCustom) {
  const badge = document.getElementById("badge-template-status");
  if (hasCustom && hasCustom.trim() !== "") {
    badge.textContent = "💻 Custom HTML Active";
    badge.style.color = "var(--gold)";
    badge.style.borderColor = "var(--gold)";
  } else {
    badge.textContent = "🏛️ Master Father Template Active";
    badge.style.color = "var(--muted)";
    badge.style.borderColor = "var(--border)";
  }
}

function onLiveEdit() {
  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  const customHtmlVal = document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value;

  liveDripMap[currentMonth] = {
    month: currentMonth,
    subject: document.getElementById("inp-subject").value.trim(),
    theme: document.getElementById("inp-theme").value.trim(),
    message: document.getElementById("inp-msg").value.trim(),
    scripture: document.getElementById("inp-quote").value.trim(),
    highlight_label: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_img: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_label_2: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_img_2: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value.trim() : "",
    custom_html: customHtmlVal
  };

  updateStatusBadge(customHtmlVal);
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  renderPreview();
}

function cleanQuoteString(raw) {
  if (!raw) return "";
  return raw.replace(/^["'“](.*)["'”]$/, '$1').replace(/^["'“]/, '').replace(/["'”]$/, '').trim();
}

function renderPreview() {
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};
  const monthLabel = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);
  const iframe = document.getElementById("preview-iframe");
  if (!iframe) return;

  const name = "Elder Smith";
  const message = (data.message || "").replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
  const cleanedQuote = cleanQuoteString(data.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.");
  const theme = data.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland";
  const points = 2;

  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  const h1Name = data.highlight_label || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value.trim();
  const h1Img = data.highlight_img || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value.trim();
  const h2Name = data.highlight_label_2 || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value.trim();
  const h2Img = data.highlight_img_2 || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value.trim();

  const items = [];
  if (h1Name) items.push({ name: h1Name, img: h1Img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE" });
  if (h2Name) items.push({ name: h2Name, img: h2Img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ" });

  let highlightHtml = '';
  if (items.length > 0) {
    highlightHtml = `
      <span style="font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 8px; letter-spacing: 2px; text-transform: uppercase; color: #8b1a1a; font-weight: bold; display: block; margin-bottom: 8px;">⭐ Product${items.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} of the Month</span>
      <table width="100%" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-bottom: 8px;">
        <tr>
          ${items.map((it, idx) => `
            <td align="center" width="${items.length === 2 ? '48%' : '100%'}" valign="top">
              <img src="${it.img}" style="width: 100%; max-width: 130px; aspect-ratio: 1/1; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 4px; border: 1px solid #c9a84c; display: block; margin: 0 auto 6px auto;" alt="${it.name}">
              <div style="font-family: 'Syne', sans-serif; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold; color: #1a1a1a;">${it.name}</div>
            </td>
            ${idx === 0 && items.length === 2 ? '<td width="4%"></td>' : ''}
          `).join('')}
        </tr>
      </table>
      <a href="https://m.me/timelesscreations.06" target="_blank" style="display: inline-block; padding: 8px 18px; background: #8b1a1a; color: #fff !important; text-decoration: none; font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 3px;">Inquire Item</a>
    `;
  }

  const rewardSectionHtml = `
    <div style="padding: 16px 12px; background-color: #fdfaf3; border: 1px solid #c9a84c; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 20px;">
      <div style="font-family: 'Syne', sans-serif; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: bold; color: #8b1a1a; margin-bottom: 4px;">🎁 Your TCRP Reward Balance</div>
      <div style="font-size: 11.5px; color: #555; margin-bottom: 12px;">You currently have <strong>2</strong> Points.</div>
      <div style="background:#fff; border:1px solid #e0d6bc; border-radius:6px; padding:12px; text-align:center; margin-bottom:12px;">
        <span style="font-size:9.5px; color:#8b1a1a; font-weight:bold; letter-spacing:1px; text-transform:uppercase; display:block; margin-bottom:6px;">🔥 You're so close! Next Goal:</span>
        <img src="https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png" style="width:55px; height:55px; aspect-ratio:1/1; object-fit:cover; border-radius:4px; display:block; margin:0 auto 6px auto; border:1px solid #d4c197;" alt="Reward">
        <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:11.5px; color:#1a1a1a;">Temple Keychain (6 PTS)</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#8c7e5d; margin-top:4px; font-weight:bold;">⚡ Only <strong>4 more points</strong> needed to claim!</div>
      </div>
      <a href="https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP" target="_blank" style="display: block; width: 88%; margin: 0 auto; padding: 10px 8px; background-color: #1a1a1a; color: #d4c197 !important; text-decoration: none; font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 3px;">
        Redeem Rewards (m.me/TimelessCreationsRP)
      </a>
    </div>
  `;

  let sourceTemplate = (data.custom_html && data.custom_html.trim() !== "") ? data.custom_html : masterTemplateCode;

  let finalHtml = sourceTemplate
    .replace(/{{MONTH}}/g, monthLabel)
    .replace(/{{NAME}}/g, name)
    .replace(/{{MESSAGE}}/g, message)
    .replace(/{{SCRIPTURE}}/g, cleanedQuote)
    .replace(/{{THEME}}/g, theme)
    .replace(/{{POINTS}}/g, String(points))
    .replace(/{{HIGHLIGHT_DISPLAY}}/g, isHigh && items.length > 0 ? "display:block" : "display:none")
    .replace(/{{HIGHLIGHT_ITEMS_HTML}}/g, highlightHtml)
    .replace(/{{REWARD_SECTION_HTML}}/g, rewardSectionHtml)
    .replace(/{{ESS1_NAME}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-ess1-name").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS1_IMG}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-img-ess1").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS2_NAME}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-ess2-name").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS2_IMG}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-img-ess2").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{GALLERY_URL}}/g, "https://photos.app.goo.gl/6h7UPfkHU5TuvzXU7");

  for (let i = 1; i <= 9; i++) {
    const gImg = document.getElementById(`inp-grid-${i}`)?.value || "";
    finalHtml = finalHtml.replace(new RegExp(`{{GRID${i}}}`, 'g'), gImg);
  }

  const doc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow.document;
  doc.open();
  doc.write(finalHtml);
  doc.close();
}

async function saveActiveMonth(sectionName = 'global') {
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};
  const label = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "save_drip",
        month: currentMonth,
        subject: data.subject,
        theme: data.theme,
        scripture: data.scripture,
        message: data.message,
        highlight_img: data.highlight_img,
        highlight_label: data.highlight_label,
        highlight_label_2: data.highlight_label_2,
        highlight_img_2: data.highlight_img_2,
        custom_html: data.custom_html
      })
    });
    const resData = await res.json();
    if (resData.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`✓ Saved ${sectionName.toUpperCase()} for ${label} to Database!`);
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(resData.error || "Failed to save.", "error");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network error saving to Turso.", "error");
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('drips', '24-Month Drips & Media');
  initSelector();
  await fetchFromTurso();
  onSelectMonth(1);
});

=== FILE: ./public/sw.js ===

const CACHE_NAME = 'tcrp-cache-v5';
const PRECACHE_ASSETS = [
  '/',
  '/index.html',
  '/manifest.json',
  '/assets/app.css',
  '/assets/app.js',
  '/pusher.html',
  '/invoicing.html',
  '/claims.html',
  '/drips.html',
  '/gallery.html',
  '/messengerbot.html',
  '/missionaries.html',
  '/changelog.html',
  '/settings.html',
  '/logs.html'
];

self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  self.skipWaiting();
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => cache.addAll(PRECACHE_ASSETS))
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(
      keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE_NAME).map(k => caches.delete(k))
    ))
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request).then(response => {
      const clone = response.clone();
      caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => cache.put(event.request, clone));
      return response;
    }).catch(() => caches.match(event.request))
  );
});
-e

<script>
let localItems = [];
let promoCodesList = [];

async function syncPersistentMenu() {
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Syncing Persistent Menu with Meta Graph API...");
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=setup_messenger_profile", { method: "POST" });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Persistent Menu synced with Facebook!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Notice: " + (d.error || "Sync error"), "error");
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network error syncing menu.", "error");
  }
}

function switchBotTab(tab) {
  if (tab === 'rewards') {
    document.getElementById('pane-rewards').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('pane-promos').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('tab-rewards-btn').className = 'btn btn-gold';
    document.getElementById('tab-promos-btn').className = 'btn btn-secondary';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('pane-rewards').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('pane-promos').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('tab-rewards-btn').className = 'btn btn-secondary';
    document.getElementById('tab-promos-btn').className = 'btn btn-gold';
    loadPromoCodes();
  }
}

function markDirty() {
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
}

async function loadInitialData(forceFetch = false) {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('temp_products', null);
  if (cached && !forceFetch) {
    localItems = cached;
    renderGrid();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_products&type=reward");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      localItems = data.products || [];
      LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
      renderGrid();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById("products-grid").innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">Error loading items.</div>';
  }
}

function renderGrid() {
  const grid = document.getElementById("products-grid");
  if (!localItems.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No reward products. Click "+ Add New Reward" above to create one.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = localItems.map((p, idx) => `
    <div class="card" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; margin-bottom:0;">
      <div class="card-img-wrap">
        <img src="${p.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" alt="${p.name}">
      </div>
      <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:4px;">${p.name}</div>
      <div style="color:var(--gold); font-size:0.72rem; font-weight:bold;">⭐ ${p.price || 0} PTS</div>
      <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px;">
        <button class="btn btn-dark" style="flex:1;" onclick="openProductModal(${idx})">✏️ Edit</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="deleteLocalItem(${idx})">🗑️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function openProductModal(idx = null) {
  if (idx !== null && localItems[idx]) {
    const p = localItems[idx];
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = idx;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = p.name || "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = p.price || 0;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = p.image_url || "";
    document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = p.image_url || "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Edit Reward Item";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Add New Reward Item";
  }
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeProductModal() {
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function openBotGalleryPicker() {
  const modal = document.getElementById("bot-gallery-modal");
  const grid = document.getElementById("bot-gallery-grid");
  modal.style.display = "flex";

  const items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', []);
  if (!items.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; padding:20px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No images in CDN gallery yet.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = items.map(it => `
    <div class="modal-img-card" onclick="selectBotImage('${it.direct_url}')" title="${it.filename}">
      <img src="${it.direct_url}" alt="${it.filename}" loading="lazy">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function selectBotImage(url) {
  document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = url;
  document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = url;
  document.getElementById("bot-gallery-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function revertBotDefault() {
  const def = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
  document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = def;
  document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = def;
}

function applyModalChanges() {
  const idx = document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value;
  const name = document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value.trim();
  const price = parseInt(document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value, 10) || 0;
  const image_url = document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value.trim();

  if (!name) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Reward item name is required.", "error");
    return;
  }

  if (idx !== "") {
    localItems[Number(idx)] = { name, price, image_url };
  } else {
    localItems.push({ name, price, image_url });
  }

  closeProductModal();
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
}

function deleteLocalItem(idx) {
  localItems.splice(idx, 1);
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
}

async function saveAllToTurso() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-sync-turso");
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Syncing to Turso...";
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "sync_catalog", type: "reward", products: localItems })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Reward catalog saved to Turso database!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(data.error || "Failed to save.", "error");
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('messengerbot', 'Messenger Bot Rewards');
  loadInitialData();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

let state = {

function showLoading(msg = "Processing request...") {
  document.getElementById('loading-text').textContent = msg;
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
}

function hideLoading() {
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'none';
}

function debounce(func, wait = 250) {
  let timeout;
  return function(...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}

window.loadRoster = loadRoster;
function loadRoster() {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  if (cached.length > 0) {
    state.missionaries = cached;
    document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = cached.length.toLocaleString();
    applyFilterAndRender();
  } else {
    triggerGlobalRefresh();
  }
}

window.onPageDataRefreshed = () => {
  state.missionaries = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  applyFilterAndRender();
};

function toggleSelectMode() {
  state.selectMode = !state.selectMode;
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggle-select-mode');
  const toolbar = document.getElementById('batch-toolbar');
  const root = document.getElementById('body-root');

  if (state.selectMode) {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: ON";
    btn.classList.add("active");
    root.classList.add("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.add("active");
  } else {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: OFF";
    btn.classList.remove("active");
    root.classList.remove("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.remove("active");
    state.selectedEmails.clear();
    updateSelectedCount();
  }
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function selectAllVisible() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function selectCurrentCohort() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    if (state.currentFilter === 'all' || (state.currentFilter === 'sister' && isSister) || (state.currentFilter === 'elder' && !isSister)) {
      state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase());
    }
  });
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function deselectAll() {
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function toggleCheckbox(email, e) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  const cleanEmail = email.toLowerCase();
  if (state.selectedEmails.has(cleanEmail)) {
    state.selectedEmails.delete(cleanEmail);
  } else {
    state.selectedEmails.add(cleanEmail);
  }
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function updateSelectedCount() {
  document.getElementById('selected-count-label').textContent = `${state.selectedEmails.size} Selected`;
}

function filterType(type, btn) {
  state.currentFilter = type;
  state.page = 1;
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(b => {
    if (!b.id.includes('toggle-select-mode')) b.classList.remove('active');
  });
  btn.classList.add('active');
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

function handleSortChange(sortVal) {
  state.currentSort = sortVal;
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

const handleDebouncedSearch = debounce((val) => {
  state.searchQuery = val.toLowerCase().trim();
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
});

document.getElementById('search-box').addEventListener('input', (e) => handleDebouncedSearch(e.target.value));

function applyFilterAndRender() {
  let list = [...state.missionaries];

  if (state.currentFilter === 'elder') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('elder') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('elder'));
  } else if (state.currentFilter === 'sister') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister'));
  }

  if (state.searchQuery) {
    list = list.filter(m =>
      (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.referral_code || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery)
    );
  }

  list.sort((a, b) => {
    if (state.currentSort === 'name_asc') {
      return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'name_desc') {
      return (b.name || '').localeCompare(a.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_desc') {
      return (Number(b.points) || 0) - (Number(a.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_asc') {
      return (Number(a.points) || 0) - (Number(b.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'oldest') {
      return (a.rowid || a.id || 0) - (b.rowid || b.id || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'newest') {
      return (b.rowid || b.id || 0) - (a.rowid || a.id || 0);
    }
    return 0;
  });

  state.filteredList = list;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function changePage(delta) {
  state.page += delta;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

async function deleteMissionary(email, name) {
  if (!confirm(`Permanently remove ${name} (${email}) from database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Deleting ${name}...`);
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Removed ${name} from roster.`);
      state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => m.email.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase());
      LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
      document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
      applyFilterAndRender();
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to delete missionary", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Network error: " + err.message, "error");
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function promptBulkAddPoints() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  const pts = prompt(`Enter points to add (or subtract using negative numbers) for ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries:`, "5");
  if (pts === null) return;
  const delta = parseInt(pts, 10);
  if (isNaN(delta)) {
    showToast("Invalid number.", "error");
    return;
  }

  showLoading(`Updating points for ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "update_missionary_points", email, delta })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        successCount++;
        const m = state.missionaries.find(x => x.email.toLowerCase() === email);
        if (m) m.points = Math.max(0, (Number(m.points) || 0) + delta);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully updated points for ${successCount} missionaries!`);
}

async function promptBulkDelete() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  if (!confirm(`Permanently delete ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries from the database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Bulk deleting ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) successCount++;
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => !state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  updateSelectedCount();
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully deleted ${successCount} missionaries.`);
}

function renderPaginatedCards() {
  const totalItems = state.filteredList.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalItems / state.pageSize) || 1;
  state.page = Math.max(1, Math.min(state.page, totalPages));

  const startIdx = (state.page - 1) * state.pageSize;
  const pageItems = state.filteredList.slice(startIdx, startIdx + state.pageSize);

  document.getElementById('showing-count').textContent = totalItems > 0 ? `${startIdx + 1}-${Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, totalItems)}` : '0';
  document.getElementById('page-indicator').textContent = `Page ${state.page} of ${totalPages}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-page-btn').disabled = state.page <= 1;
  document.getElementById('next-page-btn').disabled = state.page >= totalPages;

  const container = document.getElementById('roster-container');
  if (!pageItems.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem;">No matching missionaries found.</div>';
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = pageItems.map(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    const totalMonths = Number(m.max_months) || (isSister ? 18 : 24);
    const sentCount = Math.min(Number(m.months_sent) || 0, totalMonths);
    const pct = Math.min(Math.round((sentCount / totalMonths) * 100), 100);
    const isChecked = state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()) ? 'checked' : '';

    return `
      <div class="missionary-card">
        <div class="card-main-row">
          <input type="checkbox" class="select-checkbox" ${isChecked} onclick="toggleCheckbox('${m.email}', event)"/>
          <div class="card-summary" onclick="this.closest('.missionary-card').classList.toggle('open')">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
              <span class="cohort-pill ${isSister ? 'cohort-sister' : 'cohort-elder'}">${isSister ? 'Sister' : 'Elder'}</span>
              <span style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:700;">${m.name || 'Unnamed'}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold; background:var(--gold-dim); padding:2px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:0.7rem;">${m.points || 0} PTS</span>
              <span style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.7rem;">▼</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card-details">
          <div>✉️ <strong>Email:</strong> ${m.email}</div>
          <div>🏷️ <strong>Referral Code:</strong> ${m.referral_code || 'N/A'}</div>
          <div>🆔 <strong>Messenger PSID:</strong> ${m.psid || '<span style="color:var(--muted)">Not Linked</span>'}</div>
          <div>⚡ <strong>Status:</strong> <span style="text-transform:uppercase; color:${m.status === 'active' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'}">${m.status || 'active'}</span></div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:0.65rem; color:var(--muted); margin-top:8px;">
            <span>📩 ${sentCount} / ${totalMonths} Drip Letters</span>
            <span>${pct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div style="width:100%; height:5px; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius:99px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:3px;">
            <div style="height:100%; width:${pct}%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--green),var(--gold));"></div>
          </div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end;">
            <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteMissionary('${m.email}', '${m.name || 'Missionary'}')">🗑️ Delete Missionary</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('missionaries', 'Missionaries Roster');
  loadRoster();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
let dashboardState = { dailyStats: {} };

window.renderFromCache = renderFromCache;
function renderFromCache() {
  const cachedPayload = LocalStore.get('stats_payload', null);
  if (!cachedPayload) {
    if (typeof triggerGlobalRefresh === 'function') triggerGlobalRefresh();
    return;
  }

  const s = cachedPayload.stats || {};
  document.getElementById("stat-missionaries").textContent = (s.total_missionaries || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-points").textContent = (s.circulating_points || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-pending").textContent = (s.pending_orders || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-sent-today").textContent = (s.emails_today || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-sent-month").textContent = (s.emails_month || 0).toLocaleString();

  dashboardState.dailyStats = cachedPayload.daily_stats || {};
  renderCalendar();
}

function renderCalendar() {
  const now = new Date();
  const year = now.getFullYear();
  const month = now.getMonth();
  
  const monthEl = document.getElementById('cal-month-name');
  if (monthEl) monthEl.textContent = now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

  const container = document.getElementById('calendar-grid');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';

  const daysOfWeek = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
  daysOfWeek.forEach(d => {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'cal-day-header';
    el.textContent = d;
    container.appendChild(el);
  });

  const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay();
  const totalDays = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
  const todayDate = now.getDate();

  for (let i = 0; i < firstDay; i++) {
    const emptyCell = document.createElement('div');
    emptyCell.style.opacity = '0.04';
    emptyCell.className = 'cal-cell';
    container.appendChild(emptyCell);
  }

  for (let day = 1; day <= totalDays; day++) {
    const cell = document.createElement('div');
    const dayStr = String(day).padStart(2, '0');
    const monthStr = String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const fullDateKey = `${year}-${monthStr}-${dayStr}`;

    const count = dashboardState.dailyStats[fullDateKey] || 0;
    cell.className = `cal-cell ${day === todayDate ? 'today' : ''} ${count > 0 ? 'has-activity' : ''}`;
    
    cell.innerHTML = `
      <div class="cal-num">${day}</div>
      ${count > 0 ? `<div class="cal-count">✉ ${count}</div>` : ''}
    `;
    container.appendChild(cell);
  }
}

window.addEventListener("tcrp:data-synced", renderFromCache);
window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('dashboard', 'Dashboard');
  renderFromCache();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
