import 'dotenv/config';
import { runSql } from './lib/db.js';
import { buildCatalogCarousel, buildDashboardPayload, checkDashboardRateLimit } from './lib/bot.js';

console.log("\n🕵️ STARTING DEEP AUDIT OF ALL API HANDLERS, ENDPOINTS & VULNERABILITIES...\n");

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;
let warnings = 0;

function pass(label) {
  console.log(`✅ [PASS] ${label}`);
  passed++;
}

function fail(label, reason) {
  console.error(`❌ [FAIL] ${label} -> ${reason}`);
  failed++;
}

function warn(label, reason) {
  console.warn(`⚠️ [WARN] ${label} -> ${reason}`);
  warnings++;
}

async function runAudit() {
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 1. TURSO DATABASE INTEGRITY & SCHEMA AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("--- 1. DATABASE SCHEMA & PERMISSIONS AUDIT ---");
  try {
    await runSql(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missionaries (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        name TEXT,
        email TEXT UNIQUE,
        points INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        referral_code TEXT UNIQUE,
        fb_sender_id TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      )
    `);
    await runSql(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bot_daily_views (
        sender_id TEXT,
        view_date TEXT,
        view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        PRIMARY KEY (sender_id, view_date)
      )
    `);
    await runSql(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cdn_gallery (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        filename TEXT,
        direct_url TEXT,
        size_label TEXT,
        original_kb INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        compressed_kb INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        delete_url TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      )
    `);
    pass("Database schema verified: `missionaries`, `bot_daily_views`, and `cdn_gallery` tables exist");
  } catch (err) {
    fail("Database schema creation", err.message);
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 2. FACEBOOK GRAPH API & WEBHOOK SECURITY AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 2. FACEBOOK MESSENGER GRAPH API INTEGRITY ---");
  const fbToken = process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FACEBOOK_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  const fbVerify = process.env.VERIFY_TOKEN || process.env.FB_VERIFY_TOKEN || process.env.FACEBOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN;

  if (!fbVerify) {
    fail("Webhook Verify Token (PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN / VERIFY_TOKEN)", "Token is empty in .env. Meta Webhook verification will fail with 403.");
  } else {
    pass(`Webhook Verify Token configured (length: ${fbVerify.length})`);
  }

  if (!fbToken) {
    fail("Page Access Token (PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN / FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN)", "Missing token in .env. Bot cannot send replies back to Facebook users.");
  } else {
    try {
      const fbCheck = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me?access_token=${fbToken}`);
      const fbData = await fbCheck.json();

      if (fbData.id && fbData.name) {
        pass(`Facebook Page Access Token is ACTIVE for Page: "${fbData.name}" (ID: ${fbData.id})`);
      } else if (fbData.error) {
        fail("Facebook Page Access Token Validation", `${fbData.error.message} (Type: ${fbData.error.type})`);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      fail("Facebook Graph API Network Ping", e.message);
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 3. MESSENGER BOT PAYLOAD RECURSION & UNICODE AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 3. BOT PAYLOAD & UNICODE FORMATTING AUDIT ---");
  try {
    const testMissionary = {
      name: "Elder Allen Salviejo",
      email: "allen.salviejo@missionary.org",
      points: 5
    };
    const refLink = "https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP?ref=ALLEN77";
    const payload = buildDashboardPayload(testMissionary, refLink);

    if (payload.dashboardText.includes("**") || payload.dashboardText.includes("###")) {
      fail("Markdown Leak in Dashboard", "Found raw asterisks/hashes. Messenger will display broken syntax.");
    } else {
      pass("Dashboard Text is pure Unicode with zero raw markdown syntax");
    }

    if (!payload.dashboardText.includes("📊 𝗠𝗜𝗦𝗦𝗜𝗢𝗡𝗔𝗥𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗")) {
      fail("Unicode Header Mismatch", "Dashboard does not have the expected Unicode bold header.");
    } else {
      pass("Unicode bold mathematical headers validated");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    fail("Bot Payload Construction", err.message);
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 4. CAROUSEL & BUTTON PAYLOAD AUDIT (MAX BUTTON CHECK)
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 4. CAROUSEL & META PAYLOAD COMPLIANCE AUDIT ---");
  try {
    const mockProducts = [
      { id: 101, name: "Engraved Nametag", price: 2, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/test1.webp" },
      { id: 102, name: "POS Drip Kit", price: 8, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/test2.webp" }
    ];

    const carousel = await buildCatalogCarousel(2, mockProducts);
    const elements = carousel?.attachment?.payload?.elements;

    if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
      fail("Carousel generation", "No elements created.");
    } else {
      let buttonOverflow = false;
      elements.forEach((el, idx) => {
        if (el.buttons && el.buttons.length > 3) {
          buttonOverflow = true;
          fail(`Element ${idx + 1} Button Limit`, `Element has ${el.buttons.length} buttons. Meta strictly allows a maximum of 3 buttons per generic card.`);
        }
      });

      if (!buttonOverflow) {
        pass("All carousel cards strictly conform to Meta's max 3 button limit");
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    fail("Carousel Compliance Test", err.message);
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 5. RATE LIMITER CONCURRENCY AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 5. RATE LIMITER CONCURRENCY AUDIT ---");
  try {
    const testSender = "audit_sim_" + Date.now();
    const [res1, res2, res3] = await Promise.all([
      checkDashboardRateLimit(testSender),
      checkDashboardRateLimit(testSender),
      checkDashboardRateLimit(testSender)
    ]);

    const allowedCount = [res1, res2, res3].filter(r => r.allowed).length;
    if (allowedCount > 2) {
      warn("Race condition in Rate Limiter", "Concurrent requests bypassed limit. Use atomic transactions.");
    } else {
      pass("Daily rate limiter enforces maximum 2 views per day window");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    fail("Rate Limiter Audit", err.message);
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 6. IMGBB CDN API AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 6. IMGBB CDN HOSTING AUDIT ---");
  const imgbbKey = process.env.IMGBB_API_KEY;
  if (!imgbbKey) {
    fail("ImgBB API Key", "IMGBB_API_KEY is not defined in .env");
  } else {
    try {
      const test1pxBase64 = "R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7";
      const formData = new URLSearchParams();
      formData.append("image", test1pxBase64);
      formData.append("name", "tcrp_audit_ping");

      const imgRes = await fetch(`https://api.imgbb.com/1/upload?key=${imgbbKey}`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: formData
      });
      const imgData = await imgRes.json();

      if (imgData.success && imgData.data?.url) {
        pass(`ImgBB upload test succeeded: Direct URL active (${imgData.data.url})`);
      } else {
        fail("ImgBB Upload Verification", imgData.error?.message || "Rejected by ImgBB API");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      fail("ImgBB API Network Ping", err.message);
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------
  // 7. BREVO API AUDIT
  // ----------------------------------------------------
  console.log("\n--- 7. BREVO MAILER CONFIGURATION AUDIT ---");
  const brevoKey = process.env.BREVO_API_KEY;
  if (!brevoKey) {
    fail("Brevo API Key", "BREVO_API_KEY is missing.");
  } else {
    try {
      const brevoRes = await fetch("https://api.brevo.com/v3/account", {
        headers: { "api-key": brevoKey }
      });
      const bData = await brevoRes.json();
      if (bData.email) {
        pass(`Brevo Mailer is authenticated for account: "${bData.email}" (Plan: ${bData.plan?.[0]?.type || 'Standard'})`);
      } else {
        fail("Brevo API Authentication", bData.message || "Invalid Key");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      fail("Brevo API Connection", err.message);
    }
  }

  console.log("\n=======================================================");
  console.log(`AUDIT COMPLETE: ${passed} PASSED | ${failed} FAILED | ${warnings} WARNINGS`);
  console.log("=======================================================\n");

  if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
}

  echo "Enter your GitHub repository name (e.g., PollsPartyBotV2) [Default: $DEFAULT_REPO]:"

    input_repo="$DEFAULT_REPO"
  fi

  if [ -n "$GITHUB_TOKEN" ]; then
    REMOTE_URL="https://${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_USER}/${input_repo}.git"
  else
    REMOTE_URL="https://github.com/${GITHUB_USER}/${input_repo}.git"
  fi

  git remote add origin "$REMOTE_URL"
  echo "🔗 Linked remote origin to: github.com/${GITHUB_USER}/${input_repo}"
fi

echo "📦 Enter your commit message (or press enter for default):"
read commit_msg

if [ -z "$commit_msg" ]; then
  commit_msg="feat: update dashboard, pusher, and receipt card generation"
fi

git commit -m "$commit_msg"

echo "⬆️ Pushing to GitHub..."
git push origin main

echo "🎉 Successfully pushed to GitHub!"
read -p "Press Enter to close..."

=== FILE: ./scripts/gitbranch.js ===

const { execSync } = require('child_process');
const readline = require('readline');

const rl = readline.createInterface({
  input: process.stdin,
  output: process.stdout
});

function runGit(command) {
  try {
    return execSync(command, { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}

function showMenu() {
  console.clear();
  console.log("==========================================");
  console.log("       Git Quick Save & Load Menu         ");
  console.log("==========================================");
  console.log(" [1] Quick Load (Checkout target commit)");
  console.log(" [2] Quick Save (Commit current changes)");
  console.log("------------------------------------------");
  console.log(" [3] Save Slot 1");
  console.log(" [4] Save Slot 2");
  console.log(" [5] Save Slot 3");
  console.log(" [6] Save Slot 4");
  console.log(" [7] Save Slot 5");
  console.log(" [8] Save Slot 6");
  console.log(" [9] Save Slot 7");
  console.log(" [10] Save Slot 8");
  console.log(" [11] Save Slot 9");
  console.log("------------------------------------------");
  console.log(" [0] Exit");
  console.log("==========================================");

  rl.question("\nChoose an option (0-11): ", (answer) => {
    handleChoice(answer.trim());
  });
}

function handleChoice(choice) {
  switch (choice) {
    case '1':
      rl.question("Enter the commit hash or branch name to load: ", (target) => {
        if (target) {
          console.log(`\nSwitching to ${target}...`);
          const res = runGit(`git checkout ${target}`);
          console.log(res !== null ? "Success!" : "Error: Could not switch.");
        }
        promptReturn();
      });
      break;

    case '2':
      rl.question("Enter your quick save message: ", (msg) => {
        const message = msg.trim() || "Quick save snapshot";
        console.log("\nSaving changes...");
        runGit("git add .");
        const res = runGit(`git commit -m "${message}"`);
        console.log(res !== null ? "Successfully saved!" : "Nothing to commit or error occurred.");
        promptReturn();
      });
      break;

    // Slots 1 to 9 (Mapped to choices 3 through 11)
    case '3': case '4': case '5': case '6': case '7': 
    case '8': case '9': case '10': case '11':
      const slotNum = parseInt(choice) - 2; // Converts choice 3->1, 4->2, etc.
      handleSaveSlot(slotNum);
      break;

    case '0':
      console.log("Goodbye!");
      rl.close();
      break;

    default:
      console.log("Invalid option. Please try again.");
      setTimeout(showMenu, 1500);
      break;
  }
}

function handleSaveSlot(slot) {
  console.clear();
  console.log(`--- Save Slot ${slot} ---`);
  console.log(" [1] Save current progress into this slot (Tag it)");
  console.log(" [2] Load/Jump to this slot");
  console.log(" [3] Back to main menu");

  rl.question("\nChoose action for slot: ", (action) => {
    const tagName = `save-slot-${slot}`;
    if (action === '1') {
      // Save/Overwrite slot by moving or creating a git tag on the current HEAD
      runGit(`git tag -f ${tagName}`);
      console.log(`\nSuccessfully saved current state to Slot ${slot} (Tag: ${tagName})!`);
      promptReturn();
    } else if (action === '2') {
      // Load slot
      console.log(`\nLoading Slot ${slot}...`);
      const res = runGit(`git checkout ${tagName}`);
      console.log(res !== null ? `Successfully loaded Slot ${slot}!` : `Slot ${slot} is empty/does not exist yet.`);
      promptReturn();
    } else {
      showMenu();
    }
  });
}

function promptReturn() {
  rl.question("\nPress Enter to go back to the menu...", () => {
    showMenu();
  });
}

// Start the script
showMenu();

import 'dotenv/config';

const TOKEN = (process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || '').replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '').trim();

async function setupMessenger() {
  if (!TOKEN) {
    console.error("❌ PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN is missing!");
    return;
  }

  // 1. Get the actual Page ID associated with this token
  console.log("Fetching Page ID...");
  const meRes = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me?access_token=${TOKEN}`);
  const meData = await meRes.json();
  
  if (!meData.id) {
    console.error("❌ Failed to resolve Page ID from token:", meData);
    return;
  }

  const pageId = meData.id;
  console.log(`✅ Found Page ID: ${pageId}. Registering Persistent Menu & Get Started button...`);

  // 2. Set Get Started Button & Persistent Menu using the direct Page ID path
  const profilePayload = {
    get_started: { payload: "GET_STARTED" },
    persistent_menu: [
      {
        locale: "default",
        composer_input_disabled: false,
        call_to_actions: [
          { type: "postback", title: "🎁 Dashboard & Rewards", payload: "MENU_DASHBOARD" },
          { type: "postback", title: "❓ FAQs & Help", payload: "MENU_FAQS" },
          { type: "web_url", title: "💬 Support Inbox", url: "https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP", whitelisted_domains: ["https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP"] }
        ]
      }
    ]
  };

  const res = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/${pageId}/messenger_profile?access_token=${TOKEN}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(profilePayload)
  });

  const data = await res.json();
  console.log("Messenger Profile Setup Response:", data);
}

setupMessenger();

import 'dotenv/config';

const PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN = process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN;

if (!PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN) {
  console.error("❌ Error: Missing PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN in environment.");
  process.exit(1);
}

async function setupPersistentMenu() {
  const url = `https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me/custom_user_settings?access_token=${PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN}`;

  const payload = {
    persistent_menu: [
      {
        locale: "default",
        composer_input_disabled: false,
        call_to_actions: [
          {
            type: "postback",
            title: "📊 My Dashboard",
            payload: "ACTION_DASHBOARD"
          },
          {
            type: "postback",
            title: "🛍️ Rewards Catalog",
            payload: "DISCOVER_PAYLOAD"
          },
          {
            type: "postback",
            title: "📖 Help & FAQs",
            payload: "FAQS_PAYLOAD"
          }
        ]
      }
    ]
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.result === "success") {
      console.log("✅ Messenger Persistent Menu configured successfully!");
    } else {
      console.log("Response:", data);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Failed to setup persistent menu:", err.message);
  }
}

=== FILE: ./scripts/sync-csv.js ===

import fs from 'fs';

if (fs.existsSync('.env')) {
  const envConfig = fs.readFileSync('.env', 'utf8');
  for (const line of envConfig.split('\n')) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith('#') && trimmed.includes('=')) {
      const idx = trimmed.indexOf('=');
      const key = trimmed.substring(0, idx).trim();
      let val = trimmed.substring(idx + 1).trim();
      if ((val.startsWith('"') && val.endsWith('"')) || (val.startsWith("'") && val.endsWith("'"))) {
        val = val.substring(1, val.length - 1);
      }
      process.env[key] = val;
    }
  }
}

const tursoUrl = process.env.TURSO_DATABASE_URL;
const tursoToken = process.env.TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN;

if (!tursoUrl || !tursoToken) {
  console.error("❌ Missing TURSO_DATABASE_URL or TURSO_AUTH_TOKEN in .env");
  process.exit(1);
}

const httpUrl = tursoUrl.replace('libsql://', 'https://') + '/v2/pipeline';

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
  "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"
];

const MONTH_MAP = {
  january: 1, february: 2, march: 3, april: 4, may: 5, june: 6,
  july: 7, august: 8, september: 9, october: 10, november: 11, december: 12
};

function formatStrictMMMMYYYY(rawDate) {
  if (!rawDate) return '';
  const str = rawDate.trim();
  for (const month of MONTH_NAMES) {
    if (str.toLowerCase().includes(month.toLowerCase())) {
      const yearMatch = str.match(/\b(20\d\d)\b/);
      if (yearMatch) {
        return `${month} ${yearMatch[1]}`;
      }
    }
  }
  return str;
}

// Calculate elapsed months from batch month to current date
function computeMonthsSent(batchMonthStr, maxMonths) {
  if (!batchMonthStr) return 0;
  const s = batchMonthStr.toLowerCase();
  
  let startMonth = 0;
  for (const [name, num] of Object.entries(MONTH_MAP)) {
    if (s.includes(name)) {
      startMonth = num;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!startMonth) return 0;

  const now = new Date();
  const currentYear = now.getFullYear();
  const currentMonth = now.getMonth() + 1;

  const yearMatch = s.match(/\b(20\d\d)\b/);
  const startYear = yearMatch ? parseInt(yearMatch[1], 10) : currentYear;

  const elapsed = (currentYear - startYear) * 12 + (currentMonth - startMonth);
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(elapsed, maxMonths));
}

function parseCSV(text) {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim().length > 0);
  if (lines.length === 0) return [];

  const headers = splitCSVLine(lines[0]);
  const results = [];

  for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const fields = splitCSVLine(lines[i]);
    const obj = {};
    for (let j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) {
      obj[headers[j]] = fields[j] || '';
    }
    obj['__first_col__'] = fields[0] || '';
    results.push(obj);
  }
  return results;
}

function splitCSVLine(line) {
  const fields = [];
  let curr = '';
  let inQuotes = false;

  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
    const char = line[i];
    if (char === '"' || char === "'") {
      inQuotes = !inQuotes;
    } else if (char === ',' && !inQuotes) {
      fields.push(curr.trim());
      curr = '';
    } else {
      curr += char;
    }
  }
  fields.push(curr.trim());
  return fields;
}

async function tursoBatchQuery(statements) {
  if (statements.length === 0) return;

  const requests = statements.map(stmt => ({
    type: "execute",
    stmt: {
      sql: stmt.sql,
      args: stmt.args.map(val => (val === null ? { type: "null" } : { type: "text", value: String(val) }))
    }
  }));
  requests.push({ type: "close" });

  const response = await fetch(httpUrl, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${tursoToken}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ requests })
  });

  const data = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Turso Error ${response.status}: ${JSON.stringify(data)}`);
  }
  return data;
}

async function runCsvSync() {
  console.log("⚡ Syncing Recipients with 'batch_month' & 'months_sent' to Turso...");

  await fetch(httpUrl, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${tursoToken}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      requests: [
        { type: "execute", stmt: { sql: "DROP TABLE IF EXISTS recipients" } },
        {
          type: "execute",
          stmt: {
            sql: `CREATE TABLE recipients (
              id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
              email TEXT UNIQUE,
              name TEXT,
              last_name TEXT,
              cohort TEXT,
              max_months INTEGER,
              months_sent INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
              status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',
              batch_month TEXT
            )`
          }
        },
        { type: "close" }
      ]
    })
  });

  const files = [
    { path: 'TC Ver2 - Emails (Elders).csv', cohort: 'elder', maxMonths: 24 },
    { path: 'TC Ver2 - Emails (Sisters).csv', cohort: 'sister', maxMonths: 18 }
  ];

  for (const fileInfo of files) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(fileInfo.path)) {
      console.log(`⚠️ File not found: ${fileInfo.path}. Skipping.`);
      continue;
    }

    console.log(`📂 Processing ${fileInfo.path}...`);
    const fileContent = fs.readFileSync(fileInfo.path, 'utf8');
    const records = parseCSV(fileContent);

    let batchStatements = [];
    let totalSynced = 0;

    for (const r of records) {
      const email = (r.Email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!email || !email.endsWith('@missionary.org')) continue;

      const firstName = (r['First Name'] || '').trim();
      const lastName = (r['Last Name'] || '').trim();
      
      const rawDate = r['Start Date'] || r['__first_col__'] || '';
      const batchMonth = formatStrictMMMMYYYY(rawDate);
      const monthsSent = computeMonthsSent(batchMonth, fileInfo.maxMonths);

      const isUnsub = (r['Unsubscribe'] || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'YES';
      const status = isUnsub ? 'unsubscribed' : 'active';
      const id = 'rec_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15) + Date.now().toString(36);

      batchStatements.push({
        sql: `
          INSERT INTO recipients (id, email, name, last_name, cohort, max_months, months_sent, status, batch_month)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `,
        args: [id, email, firstName, lastName, fileInfo.cohort, fileInfo.maxMonths, monthsSent, status, batchMonth]
      });

      if (batchStatements.length >= 50) {
        try {
          await tursoBatchQuery(batchStatements);
          totalSynced += batchStatements.length;
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn(`⚠️ Batch warning: ${err.message}`);
        }
        batchStatements = [];
      }
    }

    if (batchStatements.length > 0) {
      try {
        await tursoBatchQuery(batchStatements);
        totalSynced += batchStatements.length;
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn(`⚠️ Final batch warning: ${err.message}`);
      }
    }

    console.log(`✅ Synced ${fileInfo.path}: ${totalSynced} records.`);
  }

  console.log("\n🎉 Database update complete with accurate 'months_sent' tracking!");
}

runCsvSync().catch(console.error);

<script>
let dashboardState = { dailyStats: {} };

window.renderFromCache = renderFromCache;
function renderFromCache() {
  const cachedPayload = LocalStore.get('stats_payload', null);
  if (!cachedPayload) {
    if (typeof triggerGlobalRefresh === 'function') triggerGlobalRefresh();
    return;
  }

  const s = cachedPayload.stats || {};
  document.getElementById("stat-missionaries").textContent = (s.total_missionaries || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-points").textContent = (s.circulating_points || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-pending").textContent = (s.pending_orders || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-sent-today").textContent = (s.emails_today || 0).toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById("stat-sent-month").textContent = (s.emails_month || 0).toLocaleString();

  dashboardState.dailyStats = cachedPayload.daily_stats || {};
  renderCalendar();
}

function renderCalendar() {
  const now = new Date();
  const year = now.getFullYear();
  const month = now.getMonth();
  
  const monthEl = document.getElementById('cal-month-name');
  if (monthEl) monthEl.textContent = now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

  const container = document.getElementById('calendar-grid');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';

  const daysOfWeek = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
  daysOfWeek.forEach(d => {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'cal-day-header';
    el.textContent = d;
    container.appendChild(el);
  });

  const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay();
  const totalDays = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
  const todayDate = now.getDate();

  for (let i = 0; i < firstDay; i++) {
    const emptyCell = document.createElement('div');
    emptyCell.style.opacity = '0.04';
    emptyCell.className = 'cal-cell';
    container.appendChild(emptyCell);
  }

  for (let day = 1; day <= totalDays; day++) {
    const cell = document.createElement('div');
    const dayStr = String(day).padStart(2, '0');
    const monthStr = String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const fullDateKey = `${year}-${monthStr}-${dayStr}`;

    const count = dashboardState.dailyStats[fullDateKey] || 0;
    cell.className = `cal-cell ${day === todayDate ? 'today' : ''} ${count > 0 ? 'has-activity' : ''}`;
    
    cell.innerHTML = `
      <div class="cal-num">${day}</div>
      ${count > 0 ? `<div class="cal-count">✉ ${count}</div>` : ''}
    `;
    container.appendChild(cell);
  }
}

window.addEventListener("tcrp:data-synced", renderFromCache);
window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('dashboard', 'Dashboard');
  renderFromCache();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

<script>
let currentMonth = 1;
let liveDripMap = {};
let masterTemplateCode = "";
let currentTargetInputId = null;
let currentTargetThumbId = null;

const TEMPLE_IMG_URL = "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1IkagW3wWhIhfaG01mBL4wNF-1j2lP6YG";

function getCalendarMonthLabel(monthIndex) {
  const calendarNames = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
  const idx = (Number(monthIndex) - 1) % 12;
  return calendarNames[idx];
}

function switchTab(tabId, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("active"));
  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add("active");
  btn.classList.add("active");
}

function initSelector() {
  const sel = document.getElementById("month-selector");
  sel.innerHTML = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
    const opt = document.createElement("option");
    opt.value = i;
    opt.textContent = `${getCalendarMonthLabel(i)} (Month ${i})`;
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  }
}

// Open Gallery Modal Picker
async function openGalleryModal(inputId, thumbId) {
  currentTargetInputId = inputId;
  currentTargetThumbId = thumbId;
  document.getElementById("gallery-picker-modal").style.display = "flex";
  
  let items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', null);
  if (!items) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=cdn_list');
      const d = await res.json();
      items = d.gallery || d.items || [];
      LocalStore.set('tcrp_cdn_gallery', items);
    } catch (_) { items = []; }
  }
  renderModalGrid(items);
}

function closeGalleryModal() {
  document.getElementById("gallery-picker-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function renderModalGrid(items) {
  const grid = document.getElementById("modal-grid-container");
  if (!items || !items.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No images in Gallery. Upload images in CDN Gallery first!</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = items.map(it => `
    <div class="modal-img-card" onclick="selectImage('${it.direct_url}')" title="${it.filename}">
      <img src="${it.direct_url}" alt="${it.filename}" loading="lazy">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function filterModalGrid(q) {
  const clean = (q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', []);
  const filtered = items.filter(i => (i.filename || '').toLowerCase().includes(clean));
  renderModalGrid(filtered);
}

function selectImage(url) {
  if (currentTargetInputId) {
    document.getElementById(currentTargetInputId).value = url;
  }
  if (currentTargetThumbId) {
    document.getElementById(currentTargetThumbId).src = url;
  }
  closeGalleryModal();
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Image selected from gallery!");
}

function revertImage(inputId, thumbId, defaultUrl) {
  document.getElementById(inputId).value = defaultUrl;
  document.getElementById(thumbId).src = defaultUrl;
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("↺ Reverted to default image.");
}

async function fetchFromTurso() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_drips");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && Array.isArray(data.drips)) {
      data.drips.forEach(d => {
        liveDripMap[Number(d.month)] = {
          month: Number(d.month),
          subject: d.subject || `Monthly Encouragement (${getCalendarMonthLabel(d.month)}) • Timeless Creations`,
          theme: d.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
          scripture: d.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
          message: d.message || `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others during ${getCalendarMonthLabel(d.month)}.`,
          highlight_img: d.highlight_img || "",
          highlight_label: d.highlight_label || "",
          highlight_img_2: d.highlight_img_2 || "",
          highlight_label_2: d.highlight_label_2 || "",
          custom_html: d.custom_html || ""
        };
      });
    }

    masterTemplateCode = `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark">
<style>

function onSelectMonth(m) {
  currentMonth = parseInt(m, 10);
  const label = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);
  document.getElementById("unsaved-month-label").textContent = `${label} (Month ${currentMonth})`;

  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {
    month: currentMonth,
    subject: `Monthly Encouragement (${label}) • Timeless Creations`,
    theme: "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
    scripture: "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
    message: `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others during ${label}.`,
    highlight_img: "",
    highlight_label: "",
    highlight_img_2: "",
    highlight_label_2: "",
    custom_html: ""
  };

  document.getElementById("inp-subject").value = data.subject || `Monthly Encouragement (${label}) • Timeless Creations`;
  document.getElementById("inp-theme").value = data.theme || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-msg").value = data.message || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-quote").value = data.scripture || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value = data.custom_html || "";

  if ((data.highlight_label && data.highlight_label.trim() !== "") || (data.highlight_label_2 && data.highlight_label_2.trim() !== "")) {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = true;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = data.highlight_label || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value = data.highlight_img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE";
    document.getElementById("thumb-hl-1").src = data.highlight_img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = data.highlight_label_2 || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value = data.highlight_img_2 || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ";
    document.getElementById("thumb-hl-2").src = data.highlight_img_2 || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = false;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = "";
  }

  updateStatusBadge(data.custom_html);
  renderPreview();
}

function updateStatusBadge(hasCustom) {
  const badge = document.getElementById("badge-template-status");
  if (hasCustom && hasCustom.trim() !== "") {
    badge.textContent = "💻 Custom HTML Active";
    badge.style.color = "var(--gold)";
    badge.style.borderColor = "var(--gold)";
  } else {
    badge.textContent = "🏛️ Master Father Template Active";
    badge.style.color = "var(--muted)";
    badge.style.borderColor = "var(--border)";
  }
}

function onLiveEdit() {
  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  const customHtmlVal = document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value;

  liveDripMap[currentMonth] = {
    month: currentMonth,
    subject: document.getElementById("inp-subject").value.trim(),
    theme: document.getElementById("inp-theme").value.trim(),
    message: document.getElementById("inp-msg").value.trim(),
    scripture: document.getElementById("inp-quote").value.trim(),
    highlight_label: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_img: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_label_2: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_img_2: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value.trim() : "",
    custom_html: customHtmlVal
  };

  updateStatusBadge(customHtmlVal);
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  renderPreview();
}

function cleanQuoteString(raw) {
  if (!raw) return "";
  return raw.replace(/^["'“](.*)["'”]$/, '$1').replace(/^["'“]/, '').replace(/["'”]$/, '').trim();
}

function renderPreview() {
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};
  const monthLabel = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);
  const iframe = document.getElementById("preview-iframe");
  if (!iframe) return;

  const name = "Elder Smith";
  const message = (data.message || "").replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
  const cleanedQuote = cleanQuoteString(data.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.");
  const theme = data.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland";
  const points = 2;

  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  const h1Name = data.highlight_label || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value.trim();
  const h1Img = data.highlight_img || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value.trim();
  const h2Name = data.highlight_label_2 || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value.trim();
  const h2Img = data.highlight_img_2 || document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value.trim();

  const items = [];
  if (h1Name) items.push({ name: h1Name, img: h1Img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE" });
  if (h2Name) items.push({ name: h2Name, img: h2Img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ" });

  let highlightHtml = '';
  if (items.length > 0) {
    highlightHtml = `
      <span style="font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 8px; letter-spacing: 2px; text-transform: uppercase; color: #8b1a1a; font-weight: bold; display: block; margin-bottom: 8px;">⭐ Product${items.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} of the Month</span>
      <table width="100%" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-bottom: 8px;">
        <tr>
          ${items.map((it, idx) => `
            <td align="center" width="${items.length === 2 ? '48%' : '100%'}" valign="top">
              <img src="${it.img}" style="width: 100%; max-width: 130px; aspect-ratio: 1/1; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 4px; border: 1px solid #c9a84c; display: block; margin: 0 auto 6px auto;" alt="${it.name}">
              <div style="font-family: 'Syne', sans-serif; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold; color: #1a1a1a;">${it.name}</div>
            </td>
            ${idx === 0 && items.length === 2 ? '<td width="4%"></td>' : ''}
          `).join('')}
        </tr>
      </table>
      <a href="https://m.me/timelesscreations.06" target="_blank" style="display: inline-block; padding: 8px 18px; background: #8b1a1a; color: #fff !important; text-decoration: none; font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 3px;">Inquire Item</a>
    `;
  }

  const rewardSectionHtml = `
    <div style="padding: 16px 12px; background-color: #fdfaf3; border: 1px solid #c9a84c; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 20px;">
      <div style="font-family: 'Syne', sans-serif; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: bold; color: #8b1a1a; margin-bottom: 4px;">🎁 Your TCRP Reward Balance</div>
      <div style="font-size: 11.5px; color: #555; margin-bottom: 12px;">You currently have <strong>2</strong> Points.</div>
      <div style="background:#fff; border:1px solid #e0d6bc; border-radius:6px; padding:12px; text-align:center; margin-bottom:12px;">
        <span style="font-size:9.5px; color:#8b1a1a; font-weight:bold; letter-spacing:1px; text-transform:uppercase; display:block; margin-bottom:6px;">🔥 You're so close! Next Goal:</span>
        <img src="https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png" style="width:55px; height:55px; aspect-ratio:1/1; object-fit:cover; border-radius:4px; display:block; margin:0 auto 6px auto; border:1px solid #d4c197;" alt="Reward">
        <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:11.5px; color:#1a1a1a;">Temple Keychain (6 PTS)</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#8c7e5d; margin-top:4px; font-weight:bold;">⚡ Only <strong>4 more points</strong> needed to claim!</div>
      </div>
      <a href="https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP" target="_blank" style="display: block; width: 88%; margin: 0 auto; padding: 10px 8px; background-color: #1a1a1a; color: #d4c197 !important; text-decoration: none; font-family: 'Helvetica', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 3px;">
        Redeem Rewards (m.me/TimelessCreationsRP)
      </a>
    </div>
  `;

  let sourceTemplate = (data.custom_html && data.custom_html.trim() !== "") ? data.custom_html : masterTemplateCode;

  let finalHtml = sourceTemplate
    .replace(/{{MONTH}}/g, monthLabel)
    .replace(/{{NAME}}/g, name)
    .replace(/{{MESSAGE}}/g, message)
    .replace(/{{SCRIPTURE}}/g, cleanedQuote)
    .replace(/{{THEME}}/g, theme)
    .replace(/{{POINTS}}/g, String(points))
    .replace(/{{HIGHLIGHT_DISPLAY}}/g, isHigh && items.length > 0 ? "display:block" : "display:none")
    .replace(/{{HIGHLIGHT_ITEMS_HTML}}/g, highlightHtml)
    .replace(/{{REWARD_SECTION_HTML}}/g, rewardSectionHtml)
    .replace(/{{ESS1_NAME}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-ess1-name").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS1_IMG}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-img-ess1").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS2_NAME}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-ess2-name").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{ESS2_IMG}}/g, document.getElementById("inp-img-ess2").value.trim())
    .replace(/{{GALLERY_URL}}/g, "https://photos.app.goo.gl/6h7UPfkHU5TuvzXU7");

  for (let i = 1; i <= 9; i++) {
    const gImg = document.getElementById(`inp-grid-${i}`)?.value || "";
    finalHtml = finalHtml.replace(new RegExp(`{{GRID${i}}}`, 'g'), gImg);
  }

  const doc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow.document;
  doc.open();
  doc.write(finalHtml);
  doc.close();
}

function loadMasterTemplateIntoEditor() {
  document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value = masterTemplateCode;
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Loaded Master Father Template into Editor!");
}

function clearCustomHtml() {
  document.getElementById("inp-custom-html").value = "";
  onLiveEdit();
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Cleared custom HTML!");
}

function resetTab1Defaults() {
  const label = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);
  document.getElementById("inp-subject").value = `Monthly Encouragement (${label}) • Timeless Creations`;
  document.getElementById("inp-theme").value = "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland";
  document.getElementById("inp-msg").value = `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others during ${label}.`;
  document.getElementById("inp-quote").value = "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.";
  onLiveEdit();
}

function resetTab2Defaults() {
  revertImage('inp-img-ess1', 'thumb-ess1', 'https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE');
  revertImage('inp-img-ess2', 'thumb-ess2', 'https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/101jY71PjxCwiuNznTgn7Xyc0HoXwB3WQ');
  for (let i = 1; i <= 9; i++) {
    revertImage(`inp-grid-${i}`, `thumb-grid-${i}`, defaultGrids[i-1]);
  }
}

function resetTab3Defaults() {
  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = false;
  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = "";
  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = "";
  onLiveEdit();
}

async function saveActiveMonth(sectionName = 'global') {
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};
  const label = getCalendarMonthLabel(currentMonth);

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "save_drip",
        month: currentMonth,
        subject: data.subject,
        theme: data.theme,
        scripture: data.scripture,
        message: data.message,
        highlight_img: data.highlight_img,
        highlight_label: data.highlight_label,
        highlight_label_2: data.highlight_label_2,
        highlight_img_2: data.highlight_img_2,
        custom_html: data.custom_html
      })
    });
    const resData = await res.json();
    if (resData.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`✓ Saved ${sectionName.toUpperCase()} for ${label} to Database!`);
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(resData.error || "Failed to save.", "error");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network error saving to Turso.", "error");
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('drips', '24-Month Drips & Media');
  initSelector();
  await fetchFromTurso();
  onSelectMonth(1);
});
</script>

=== FILE: ./views/sw.js ===

const CACHE_NAME = 'tcrp-cache-v5';
const PRECACHE_ASSETS = [
  '/',
  '/index.html',
  '/manifest.json',
  '/assets/app.css',
  '/assets/app.js',
  '/pusher.html',
  '/invoicing.html',
  '/claims.html',
  '/drips.html',
  '/gallery.html',
  '/messengerbot.html',
  '/missionaries.html',
  '/changelog.html',
  '/settings.html',
  '/logs.html'
];

self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  self.skipWaiting();
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => cache.addAll(PRECACHE_ASSETS))
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(
      keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE_NAME).map(k => caches.delete(k))
    ))
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request).then(response => {
      const clone = response.clone();
      caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => cache.put(event.request, clone));
      return response;
    }).catch(() => caches.match(event.request))
  );
});
-e

            <li><strong>Interaction Data:</strong> Commands, message inputs, referral codes used, and rewards activity.</li>
            <li><strong>Account Metadata:</strong> Verification status, earned rewards points, total referral counts, and timestamp logs.</li>
        </ul>

        <h2>3. How We Use Your Information</h2>
        <p>Your information is used strictly for technical operation and account administration:</p>
        <ul>
            <li>To verify eligibility through official organizational email authentication.</li>
            <li>To calculate, attribute, and manage reward point balances and referral credits.</li>
            <li>To route system notifications, verification codes (OTPs), and administrative updates via integrated services like Brevo.</li>
            <li>To maintain application security, prevent unauthorized duplicate registrations, and manage administrative privileges.</li>
        </ul>

        <h2>4. Data Storage & Third-Party Services</h2>
        <p>We implement industry-standard encryption and security measures. We do not sell, rent, or trade your personal data. We share data only with verified infrastructure providers required to run this application:</p>
        <ul>
            <li><strong>Google Firebase Firestore:</strong> Cloud database infrastructure for secure real-time state and account storage.</li>
            <li><strong>Vercel Cloud Infrastructure:</strong> Serverless deployment and execution host for Messenger Webhook API endpoints.</li>
            <li><strong>Brevo (formerly Sendinblue):</strong> Transactional email service used exclusively for dispatching verification codes.</li>
            <li><strong>Meta Graph API:</strong> For receiving and responding to Facebook Messenger communications.</li>
        </ul>

        <h2>5. Data Retention & User Rights</h2>
        <p>We retain account records for as long as your account remains active within the rewards program. You have the right to request access to or deletion of your personal data at any time.</p>

        <h2>6. How to Request Data Deletion</h2>
        <p>In compliance with Meta Developer Policies, if you wish to revoke access or request full deletion of your user profile, PSID records, and earned points balance from our databases, you may do so by:</p>
        <ul>
            <li>Sending a direct message containing <code>/delete_account</code> in our Messenger chat.</li>
            <li>Contacting our Privacy Administrator directly via email at the address listed below. Requests are fulfilled within 7 business days.</li>
        </ul>

        <h2>7. Contact Information</h2>
        <div class="contact-box">
            <p>If you have questions, concerns, or requests regarding this Privacy Policy, please contact our team:</p>
            <p><strong>Application:</strong> TimelessCreationsRewardsProgram (TCRP)<br>
            <strong>Developer / Administrator:</strong> AllensCreations<br>
            <strong>Contact Email:</strong> <a href="mailto:2ndsalviejomark2019@gmail.com">2ndsalviejomark2019@gmail.com</a></p>
        </div>

        </footer>
    </div>

<body id="body-root">

  <div class="spinner"></div>
  <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; color:var(--gold); font-size:0.85rem; letter-spacing:0.08em;" id="loading-text">Processing request...</div>
</div>

<div class="main-wrap">
  <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:16px; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px;">
    <div>
      <h2 style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:1.25rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--gold);">Missionaries Roster</h2>
      <p style="font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--muted); margin-top:4px;">
        Showing <span id="showing-count">0</span> of <span id="roster-count">0</span> registered missionaries.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex; gap:8px;">
      <button class="filter-btn" id="toggle-select-mode" onclick="toggleSelectMode()">☑️ Select Mode: OFF</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="table-controls">
    <input class="search-input" id="search-box" placeholder="Search missionary name, email, or code..."/>
    <button class="filter-btn active" onclick="filterType('all', this)">All</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="filterType('elder', this)">Elders (24M)</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="filterType('sister', this)">Sisters (18M)</button>
    
    <select class="sort-select" id="sort-select" onchange="handleSortChange(this.value)">
      <option value="name_asc">Sort: Name (A-Z)</option>
      <option value="name_desc">Sort: Name (Z-A)</option>
      <option value="points_desc">Sort: Points (High to Low)</option>
      <option value="points_asc">Sort: Points (Low to High)</option>
      <option value="oldest">Sort: Oldest Added</option>
      <option value="newest">Sort: Newest Added</option>
    </select>
  </div>

  <div class="roster-list" id="roster-container">
    <div style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem;">Loading missionaries...</div>
  </div>

  <div class="pagination-bar">
    <button class="page-btn" id="prev-page-btn" onclick="changePage(-1)">← Previous</button>
    <span id="page-indicator">Page 1</span>
    <button class="page-btn" id="next-page-btn" onclick="changePage(1)">Next →</button>
  </div>

<!-- Floating Batch Toolbar -->
<div class="batch-toolbar" id="batch-toolbar">
  <div style="display:flex; gap:12px; align-items:center;">
    <span style="font-size:0.78rem; color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold;" id="selected-count-label">0 Selected</span>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="selectAllVisible()">Select All</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="selectCurrentCohort()">Select Current Cohort</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="deselectAll()">Deselect</button>
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex; gap:10px;">
    <button class="filter-btn" style="background:var(--gold-dim); color:var(--gold); border-color:var(--gold);" onclick="promptBulkAddPoints()">⭐ Bulk Add Points</button>
    <button class="btn-delete" style="margin-top:0;" onclick="promptBulkDelete()">🗑️ Bulk Delete Selected</button>
  </div>

<script>
let state = {
  missionaries: [],
  filteredList: [],
  currentFilter: 'all',
  currentSort: 'name_asc',
  searchQuery: '',
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 50,
  selectMode: false,
  selectedEmails: new Set()
};

function showLoading(msg = "Processing request...") {
  document.getElementById('loading-text').textContent = msg;
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
}

function hideLoading() {
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'none';
}

function debounce(func, wait = 250) {
  let timeout;
  return function(...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}

window.loadRoster = loadRoster;
function loadRoster() {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  if (cached.length > 0) {
    state.missionaries = cached;
    document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = cached.length.toLocaleString();
    applyFilterAndRender();
  } else {
    triggerGlobalRefresh();
  }
}

window.onPageDataRefreshed = () => {
  state.missionaries = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  applyFilterAndRender();
};

function toggleSelectMode() {
  state.selectMode = !state.selectMode;
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggle-select-mode');
  const toolbar = document.getElementById('batch-toolbar');
  const root = document.getElementById('body-root');

  if (state.selectMode) {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: ON";
    btn.classList.add("active");
    root.classList.add("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.add("active");
  } else {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: OFF";
    btn.classList.remove("active");
    root.classList.remove("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.remove("active");
    state.selectedEmails.clear();
    updateSelectedCount();
  }
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function selectAllVisible() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function selectCurrentCohort() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    if (state.currentFilter === 'all' || (state.currentFilter === 'sister' && isSister) || (state.currentFilter === 'elder' && !isSister)) {
      state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase());
    }
  });
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function deselectAll() {
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function toggleCheckbox(email, e) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  const cleanEmail = email.toLowerCase();
  if (state.selectedEmails.has(cleanEmail)) {
    state.selectedEmails.delete(cleanEmail);
  } else {
    state.selectedEmails.add(cleanEmail);
  }
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function updateSelectedCount() {
  document.getElementById('selected-count-label').textContent = `${state.selectedEmails.size} Selected`;
}

function filterType(type, btn) {
  state.currentFilter = type;
  state.page = 1;
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(b => {
    if (!b.id.includes('toggle-select-mode')) b.classList.remove('active');
  });
  btn.classList.add('active');
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

function handleSortChange(sortVal) {
  state.currentSort = sortVal;
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

const handleDebouncedSearch = debounce((val) => {
  state.searchQuery = val.toLowerCase().trim();
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
});

document.getElementById('search-box').addEventListener('input', (e) => handleDebouncedSearch(e.target.value));

function applyFilterAndRender() {
  let list = [...state.missionaries];

  if (state.currentFilter === 'elder') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('elder') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('elder'));
  } else if (state.currentFilter === 'sister') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister'));
  }

  if (state.searchQuery) {
    list = list.filter(m =>
      (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.referral_code || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery)
    );
  }

  list.sort((a, b) => {
    if (state.currentSort === 'name_asc') {
      return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'name_desc') {
      return (b.name || '').localeCompare(a.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_desc') {
      return (Number(b.points) || 0) - (Number(a.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_asc') {
      return (Number(a.points) || 0) - (Number(b.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'oldest') {
      return (a.rowid || a.id || 0) - (b.rowid || b.id || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'newest') {
      return (b.rowid || b.id || 0) - (a.rowid || a.id || 0);
    }
    return 0;
  });

  state.filteredList = list;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function changePage(delta) {
  state.page += delta;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

async function deleteMissionary(email, name) {
  if (!confirm(`Permanently remove ${name} (${email}) from database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Deleting ${name}...`);
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Removed ${name} from roster.`);
      state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => m.email.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase());
      LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
      document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
      applyFilterAndRender();
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to delete missionary", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Network error: " + err.message, "error");
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function promptBulkAddPoints() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  const pts = prompt(`Enter points to add (or subtract using negative numbers) for ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries:`, "5");
  if (pts === null) return;
  const delta = parseInt(pts, 10);
  if (isNaN(delta)) {
    showToast("Invalid number.", "error");
    return;
  }

  showLoading(`Updating points for ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "update_missionary_points", email, delta })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        successCount++;
        const m = state.missionaries.find(x => x.email.toLowerCase() === email);
        if (m) m.points = Math.max(0, (Number(m.points) || 0) + delta);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully updated points for ${successCount} missionaries!`);
}

async function promptBulkDelete() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  if (!confirm(`Permanently delete ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries from the database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Bulk deleting ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) successCount++;
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => !state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  updateSelectedCount();
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully deleted ${successCount} missionaries.`);
}

function renderPaginatedCards() {
  const totalItems = state.filteredList.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalItems / state.pageSize) || 1;
  state.page = Math.max(1, Math.min(state.page, totalPages));

  const startIdx = (state.page - 1) * state.pageSize;
  const pageItems = state.filteredList.slice(startIdx, startIdx + state.pageSize);

  document.getElementById('showing-count').textContent = totalItems > 0 ? `${startIdx + 1}-${Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, totalItems)}` : '0';
  document.getElementById('page-indicator').textContent = `Page ${state.page} of ${totalPages}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-page-btn').disabled = state.page <= 1;
  document.getElementById('next-page-btn').disabled = state.page >= totalPages;

  const container = document.getElementById('roster-container');
  if (!pageItems.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem;">No matching missionaries found.</div>';
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = pageItems.map(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    const totalMonths = Number(m.max_months) || (isSister ? 18 : 24);
    const sentCount = Math.min(Number(m.months_sent) || 0, totalMonths);
    const pct = Math.min(Math.round((sentCount / totalMonths) * 100), 100);
    const isChecked = state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()) ? 'checked' : '';

    return `
      <div class="missionary-card">
        <div class="card-main-row">
          <input type="checkbox" class="select-checkbox" ${isChecked} onclick="toggleCheckbox('${m.email}', event)"/>
          <div class="card-summary" onclick="this.closest('.missionary-card').classList.toggle('open')">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
              <span class="cohort-pill ${isSister ? 'cohort-sister' : 'cohort-elder'}">${isSister ? 'Sister' : 'Elder'}</span>
              <span style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:700;">${m.name || 'Unnamed'}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold; background:var(--gold-dim); padding:2px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:0.7rem;">${m.points || 0} PTS</span>
              <span style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.7rem;">▼</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card-details">
          <div>✉️ <strong>Email:</strong> ${m.email}</div>
          <div>🏷️ <strong>Referral Code:</strong> ${m.referral_code || 'N/A'}</div>
          <div>🆔 <strong>Messenger PSID:</strong> ${m.psid || '<span style="color:var(--muted)">Not Linked</span>'}</div>
          <div>⚡ <strong>Status:</strong> <span style="text-transform:uppercase; color:${m.status === 'active' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'}">${m.status || 'active'}</span></div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:0.65rem; color:var(--muted); margin-top:8px;">
            <span>📩 ${sentCount} / ${totalMonths} Drip Letters</span>
            <span>${pct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div style="width:100%; height:5px; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius:99px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:3px;">
            <div style="height:100%; width:${pct}%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--green),var(--gold));"></div>
          </div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end;">
            <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteMissionary('${m.email}', '${m.name || 'Missionary'}')">🗑️ Delete Missionary</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('missionaries', 'Missionaries Roster');
  loadRoster();
});
</script>

<script>
let currentPowerState = "offline";
let captchaRound = 1;

const CAPTCHA_IMAGES = [
  { id: "correct", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/mgjRv9Dy/IMG-7154.jpg", isHandsome: true },
  { id: "wrong1", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/9X92WK05/images.webp", isHandsome: false },
  { id: "wrong2", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/s21Fb0qk/images-(1).webp", isHandsome: false }
];

function initMonthPicker() {
  const sel = document.getElementById("test-drip-month");
  if (!sel) return;
  sel.innerHTML = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
    const opt = document.createElement("option");
    opt.value = i;
    opt.textContent = `Month ${i}`;
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  }
}

function toggleMonthPicker(val) {
  const pickerWrap = document.getElementById("month-picker-wrap");
  if (pickerWrap) {
    pickerWrap.style.display = val === "drip" ? "block" : "none";
  }
}

window.loadSettings = loadSettings;
async function loadSettings() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("power-toggle-btn");
  if (!btn) return;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=health_check");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      currentPowerState = "online";
      btn.className = "btn btn-gold";
      btn.textContent = "⚡ System: ONLINE (Click to Sleep)";
    } else {
      currentPowerState = "offline";
      btn.className = "btn btn-dark";
      btn.textContent = "💤 System: OFFLINE / SLEEPING (Click to Wake)";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    btn.className = "btn btn-dark";
    btn.textContent = "⚠️ Disconnected";
  }

  loadCdnSettings();
}

async function loadCdnSettings() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_cdn_config");
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok && d.config) {
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner").value = d.config.cdn_github_owner || "";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo").value = d.config.cdn_github_repo || "";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch").value = d.config.cdn_github_branch || "main";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-path")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-path").value = d.config.cdn_upload_path || "assets/rewards";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-token")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-token").value = d.config.cdn_github_token || "";
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function saveCdnSettings() {
  const payload = {
    cdn_github_owner: document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner").value.trim(),
    cdn_github_repo: document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo").value.trim(),
    cdn_github_branch: document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch").value.trim() || "main",
    cdn_upload_path: document.getElementById("set-cdn-path").value.trim() || "assets/rewards",
    cdn_github_token: document.getElementById("set-cdn-token").value.trim()
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=save_cdn_config", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("jsDelivr & GitHub variables saved!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Failed to save CDN variables", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Error saving: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

function startPowerCaptcha() {
  captchaRound = 1;
  renderCaptchaRound();
  document.getElementById("captcha-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeCaptchaModal() {
  document.getElementById("captcha-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function renderCaptchaRound() {
  const arena = document.getElementById("captcha-arena-container");
  const title = document.getElementById("captcha-round-title");
  const diffLabel = document.getElementById("captcha-difficulty-label");

  document.getElementById("p-dot-1").className = captchaRound >= 1 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";
  document.getElementById("p-dot-2").className = captchaRound >= 2 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";
  document.getElementById("p-dot-3").className = captchaRound >= 3 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";

  let animClasses = ["", "", ""];
  if (captchaRound === 1) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 1 of 3: Tap the real <strong>Handsome Face</strong> (Static)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 1 (Easy)";
  } else if (captchaRound === 2) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 2 of 3: Target is <strong>Moving!</strong> (Medium)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 2 (Medium)";
    animClasses = ["move-float-1", "move-float-2", "move-float-3"];
  } else if (captchaRound === 3) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 3 of 3: <strong>Fast Speed & Orbit!</strong> (Hard)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 3 (Hard)";
    animClasses = ["move-fast-1", "move-fast-2", "move-fast-3"];
  }

  const shuffled = [...CAPTCHA_IMAGES].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);

  arena.innerHTML = shuffled.map((item, idx) => `
    <div class="captcha-card ${animClasses[idx]}" onclick="handleCaptchaPick(${item.isHandsome})">
      <img src="${item.src}" alt="Face">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

async function handleCaptchaPick(isHandsome) {
  const box = document.getElementById("captcha-box");

  if (!isHandsome) {
    box.classList.remove("shake-box");
    void box.offsetWidth;
    box.classList.add("shake-box");
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("Wrong face selected! Resetting to Round 1.", "error");
    }
    captchaRound = 1;
    renderCaptchaRound();
    return;
  }

  if (captchaRound < 3) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(`✓ Round ${captchaRound} Passed! Speed increasing...`);
    }
    captchaRound++;
    renderCaptchaRound();
  } else {
    closeCaptchaModal();
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("🏆 All 3 Rounds Complete! Pogi Certified.");
    }
    await executePowerToggle();
  }
}

async function executePowerToggle() {
  const nextState = currentPowerState === "online" ? "offline" : "online";
  const btn = document.getElementById("power-toggle-btn");
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Saving to Turso...";
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "toggle_power", state: nextState })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
        showToast(`Master power state changed to ${nextState.toUpperCase()}`);
      }
      loadSettings();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("Failed to update power state.", "error");
    }
    loadSettings();
  }
}

async function sendTestEmail() {
  const input = document.getElementById("test-email-input").value.trim();
  const templateType = document.getElementById("test-template-select").value;
  const month = document.getElementById("test-drip-month").value;
  const consoleBox = document.getElementById("test-console");
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-send-test");

  if (!input) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Enter at least one email address.", "error");
    return;
  }

  consoleBox.style.display = "block";
  consoleBox.textContent = `⏳ Dispatching authentic [${templateType.toUpperCase()}] template from templates directory...\nSender: noreply.timelesscreations.ph@gmail.com\nTarget(s): ${input}\n\n`;
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Sending...";
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "test_email",
        email: input,
        template_type: templateType,
        month: Number(month) || 1
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    consoleBox.textContent += JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);

    if (data.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`Test dispatch successful! Delivered ${data.totalSent || 1} email(s).`);
      consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n✅ ALL DISPATCHES DELIVERED SUCCESSFULLY USING TEMPLATE FILE!";
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`Dispatch issue: ${data.error || "Review details below"}`, "error");
      consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n❌ ERROR: See diagnostic response details above.";
    }
  } catch (err) {
    consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n❌ Network Error: " + err.message;
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network execution error.", "error");
  } finally {
    btn.textContent = "🚀 Dispatch Authentic Template";
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

function openCronModal() {
  document.getElementById("modal-input-val").value = "";
  document.getElementById("custom-modal").style.display = "flex";
}
function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById("custom-modal").style.display = "none";
}
function submitCronModal() {
  const val = document.getElementById("modal-input-val").value.trim();
  if (val === "Pogi si Allen") {
    closeModal();
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Executing scheduled drip cron...");
    fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "force_cron" })
    }).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Cron execution completed successfully!");
    });
  } else {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Security phrase did not match.", "error");
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') {
    initAppLayout('settings', 'System Control Room');
  }
  initMonthPicker();
  loadSettings();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
