<script>
let localItems = [];
let promoCodesList = [];

async function syncPersistentMenu() {
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Syncing Persistent Menu with Meta Graph API...");
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=setup_messenger_profile", { method: "POST" });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Persistent Menu synced with Facebook!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Notice: " + (d.error || "Sync error"), "error");
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network error syncing menu.", "error");
  }
}

function switchBotTab(tab) {
  if (tab === 'rewards') {
    document.getElementById('pane-rewards').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('pane-promos').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('tab-rewards-btn').className = 'btn btn-gold';
    document.getElementById('tab-promos-btn').className = 'btn btn-secondary';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('pane-rewards').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('pane-promos').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('tab-rewards-btn').className = 'btn btn-secondary';
    document.getElementById('tab-promos-btn').className = 'btn btn-gold';
    loadPromoCodes();
  }
}

function markDirty() {
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
}

async function loadInitialData(forceFetch = false) {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('temp_products', null);
  if (cached && !forceFetch) {
    localItems = cached;
    renderGrid();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_products&type=reward");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      localItems = data.products || [];
      LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
      renderGrid();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById("products-grid").innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">Error loading items.</div>';
  }
}

function renderGrid() {
  const grid = document.getElementById("products-grid");
  if (!localItems.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No reward products. Click "+ Add New Reward" above to create one.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = localItems.map((p, idx) => `
    <div class="card" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; margin-bottom:0;">
      <div class="card-img-wrap">
        <img src="${p.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" alt="${p.name}">
      </div>
      <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:4px;">${p.name}</div>
      <div style="color:var(--gold); font-size:0.72rem; font-weight:bold;">⭐ ${p.price || 0} PTS</div>
      <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px;">
        <button class="btn btn-dark" style="flex:1;" onclick="openProductModal(${idx})">✏️ Edit</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="deleteLocalItem(${idx})">🗑️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function openProductModal(idx = null) {
  if (idx !== null && localItems[idx]) {
    const p = localItems[idx];
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = idx;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = p.name || "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = p.price || 0;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = p.image_url || "";
    document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = p.image_url || "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Edit Reward Item";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Add New Reward Item";
  }
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeProductModal() {
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function openBotGalleryPicker() {
  const modal = document.getElementById("bot-gallery-modal");
  const grid = document.getElementById("bot-gallery-grid");
  modal.style.display = "flex";

  const items = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', []);
  if (!items.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; padding:20px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No images in CDN gallery yet.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = items.map(it => `
    <div class="modal-img-card" onclick="selectBotImage('${it.direct_url}')" title="${it.filename}">
      <img src="${it.direct_url}" alt="${it.filename}" loading="lazy">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function selectBotImage(url) {
  document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = url;
  document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = url;
  document.getElementById("bot-gallery-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function revertBotDefault() {
  const def = "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png";
  document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = def;
  document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = def;
}

function applyModalChanges() {
  const idx = document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value;
  const name = document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value.trim();
  const price = parseInt(document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value, 10) || 0;
  const image_url = document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value.trim();

  if (!name) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Reward item name is required.", "error");
    return;
  }

  if (idx !== "") {
    localItems[Number(idx)] = { name, price, image_url };
  } else {
    localItems.push({ name, price, image_url });
  }

  closeProductModal();
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
}

function deleteLocalItem(idx) {
  localItems.splice(idx, 1);
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
}

async function saveAllToTurso() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-sync-turso");
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Syncing to Turso...";
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "sync_catalog", type: "reward", products: localItems })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Reward catalog saved to Turso database!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(data.error || "Failed to save.", "error");
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('messengerbot', 'Messenger Bot Rewards');
  loadInitialData();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

</style>

<script>
let missionaries = [];
let cashInvoices = [];
let orders = [];
let productCatalog = [];
let recipientMode = 'db';

function switchNavTab(tabId, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-pane').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('active');
}

window.loadData = loadData;
async function loadData() {
  try {
    const [mRes, invRes, prodRes, ordRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_missionaries'),
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_invoices'),
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_products&type=cash'),
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_orders')
    ]);

    const mData = await mRes.json();
    const invData = await invRes.json();
    const prodData = await prodRes.json();
    const ordData = await ordRes.json();

    missionaries = mData.missionaries || [];
    cashInvoices = invData.invoices || [];
    productCatalog = prodData.products || [];
    orders = ordData.orders || [];

    if (productCatalog.length === 0) {
      productCatalog = [
        { name: "Standard Missionary Strap", price: 150 },
        { name: "Customized Nametag", price: 250 },
        { name: "Scripture Cover Set", price: 450 }
      ];
    }

    populateMissionarySelect(missionaries);
    renderUnifiedTable();
    renderProductSettings();

    if (document.querySelectorAll('#items-tbody tr').length === 0 && productCatalog.length > 0) {
      addItemRow(productCatalog[0].name, productCatalog[0].price, 1);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Failed to load inventory data", "error");
  }
}

function updateBadgeCounts() {
  const totalCount = orders.length + cashInvoices.length;
  document.getElementById('trans-badge').textContent = totalCount;
}

function renderUnifiedTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('unified-tbody');
  updateBadgeCounts();

  let combined = [];
  
  orders.forEach(o => {
    combined.push({
      id: o.order_id,
      type: 'Reward Claim',
      name: o.name || 'Anonymous',
      email: o.email || '—',
      details: `🎁 ${o.item} (${o.points_cost} PTS)`,
      status: (o.status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase(),
      raw: o,
      isOrder: true
    });
  });

  cashInvoices.forEach(inv => {
    let summary = 'Custom POS Order';
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(inv.items_json);
      summary = parsed.map(i => `${i.qty}x ${i.name}`).join(', ');
    } catch(_) {}

    combined.push({
      id: inv.invoice_id,
      type: 'Cash Invoice',
      name: inv.name,
      email: inv.email || '—',
      details: `${summary} (₱${Number(inv.total_amount).toFixed(2)})`,
      status: (inv.status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase(),
      raw: inv,
      isOrder: false
    });
  });

  combined.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.raw.created_at || 0) - new Date(a.raw.created_at || 0));

  if (!combined.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);">No transactions or claims recorded yet.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = combined.map(item => {
    const isPaid = item.status === 'PAID' || item.status === 'COMPLETED';
    const statusClass = isPaid ? 'paid' : 'pending';
    return `
      <tr>
        <td style="font-weight:bold;color:var(--gold);">${item.id}</td>
        <td><span style="font-size:0.6rem;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--surface2);border:1px solid var(--border);">${item.type}</span></td>
        <td style="font-weight:600;">${item.name}</td>
        <td style="color:var(--muted);">${item.email}</td>
        <td>${item.details}</td>
        <td style="text-align:center;">
          <select class="status-select ${statusClass}" onchange="updateStatus('${item.id}', ${item.isOrder}, this.value)">
            <option value="PENDING" ${item.status === 'PENDING' ? 'selected' : ''}>PENDING</option>
            <option value="PAID" ${isPaid ? 'selected' : ''}>PAID</option>
            <option value="READY FOR PICKUP" ${item.status === 'READY FOR PICKUP' ? 'selected' : ''}>READY FOR PICKUP</option>
            <option value="OUT FOR DELIVERY" ${item.status === 'OUT FOR DELIVERY' ? 'selected' : ''}>OUT FOR DELIVERY</option>
            <option value="DELIVERED" ${item.status === 'DELIVERED' ? 'selected' : ''}>DELIVERED</option>
          </select>
        </td>
        <td style="text-align:center; display:flex; gap:6px; justify-content:center;">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.65rem;" onclick="openEditModal('${item.id}', ${item.isOrder})">✏️ Edit</button>
          <button class="btn-danger" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.65rem;" onclick="deleteTransaction('${item.id}', ${item.isOrder}, '${item.name}')">🗑️</button>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

async function updateStatus(id, isOrder, newStatus) {
  if (!confirm(`Confirm status change to [${newStatus}] and trigger automated notification email for ${id}?`)) {
    renderUnifiedTable();
    return;
  }

  const endpointAction = isOrder ? 'update_order_status' : 'update_invoice_status';
  const payloadKey = isOrder ? 'order_id' : 'invoice_id';

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: endpointAction, [payloadKey]: id, status: newStatus })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Transaction ${id} updated to ${newStatus}! Email dispatched.`);
      loadData();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error updating status", "error");
  }
}

async function deleteTransaction(id, isOrder, name) {
  if (!confirm(`Permanently delete transaction ${id} for ${name}?`)) return;

  const actionName = isOrder ? 'delete_order' : 'delete_invoice';
  const idKey = isOrder ? 'order_id' : 'invoice_id';

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: actionName, [idKey]: id })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Deleted ${id}`);
      loadData();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error deleting record", "error");
  }
}

// Edit Modal Functions with Confirmation Popup
function openEditModal(id, isOrder) {
  document.getElementById('edit-trx-id').value = id;
  document.getElementById('edit-trx-type').value = isOrder ? 'order' : 'invoice';

  let name = '';
  let items = [];

  if (isOrder) {
    const o = orders.find(x => x.order_id === id);
    if (!o) return;
    name = o.name;
    try { items = JSON.parse(o.items_json || '[]'); } catch(_) {
      items = [{ name: o.item, qty: 1, price: 0, subtotal: 0 }];
    }
  } else {
    const inv = cashInvoices.find(x => x.invoice_id === id);
    if (!inv) return;
    name = inv.name;
    try { items = JSON.parse(inv.items_json || '[]'); } catch(_) {}
  }

  document.getElementById('edit-trx-name').value = name;
  document.getElementById('edit-modal-title').textContent = `Edit Transaction ${id}`;

  const tbody = document.getElementById('edit-items-tbody');
  tbody.innerHTML = '';
  items.forEach(it => addEditItemRow(it.name, it.price, it.qty));

  document.getElementById('edit-transaction-modal').style.display = 'flex';
  recalculateEditTotal();
}

function closeEditModal() {
  document.getElementById('edit-transaction-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

function addEditItemRow(name = '', price = 0, qty = 1) {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('edit-items-tbody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.className = 'edit-item-row';
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td>
      <select class="form-input edit-item-select" onchange="handleEditProductChange(this)" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;">
        <option value="">-- Select Product --</option>
        ${productCatalog.map(p => `<option value="${p.name}" data-price="${p.price}" ${p.name === name ? 'selected' : ''}>${p.name} (₱${p.price})</option>`).join('')}
      </select>
      <input type="text" class="form-input edit-item-name" value="${name}" placeholder="Or custom item name..." style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;margin-top:4px;" ${name ? '' : 'style="display:none;"'}/>
    </td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input edit-item-qty" value="${qty}" min="1" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateEditTotal()"/></td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input edit-item-price" value="${price}" min="0" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateEditTotal()"/></td>
    <td style="text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:var(--gold);font-size:0.75rem;" class="edit-item-sub">₱${(price * qty).toFixed(2)}</td>
    <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove(); recalculateEditTotal();">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);
  recalculateEditTotal();
}

function handleEditProductChange(sel) {
  const opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
  const tr = sel.closest('tr');
  if (opt.value) {
    tr.querySelector('.edit-item-price').value = opt.getAttribute('data-price') || 0;
    tr.querySelector('.edit-item-name').value = opt.value;
  }
  recalculateEditTotal();
}

function recalculateEditTotal() {
  let subtotal = 0;
  document.querySelectorAll('.edit-item-row').forEach(r => {
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.edit-item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.edit-item-price').value) || 0;
    const s = qty * price;
    r.querySelector('.edit-item-sub').textContent = `₱${s.toFixed(2)}`;
    subtotal += s;
  });
  document.getElementById('edit-calc-sub').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('edit-calc-total').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
}

function promptSaveConfirmation() {
  if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to save these bundled changes and notify the customer?")) return;
  saveEditedTransaction();
}

async function saveEditedTransaction() {
  const id = document.getElementById('edit-trx-id').value;
  const isOrder = document.getElementById('edit-trx-type').value === 'order';
  const items = [];
  let subtotal = 0;

  document.querySelectorAll('.edit-item-row').forEach(r => {
    const nameInput = r.querySelector('.edit-item-name').value.trim() || r.querySelector('.edit-item-select').value.trim();
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.edit-item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.edit-item-price').value) || 0;
    const s = qty * price;
    subtotal += s;
    if (nameInput) items.push({ name: nameInput, qty, price, subtotal: s });
  });

  if (items.length === 0) {
    showToast("Transaction must contain at least one item", "error");
    return;
  }

  const actionName = isOrder ? 'update_order' : 'update_invoice';
  const idKey = isOrder ? 'order_id' : 'invoice_id';

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: actionName,
        [idKey]: id,
        items_json: items,
        subtotal,
        discount_type: 'fixed',
        discount_val: 0,
        discount_amount: 0,
        total_amount: subtotal
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Transaction ${id} successfully updated with bundled items!`);
      closeEditModal();
      loadData();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error updating transaction", "error");
  }
}

function switchRecipientMode(mode) {
  recipientMode = mode;
  document.getElementById('mode-db-btn').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'db');
  document.getElementById('mode-custom-btn').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'custom');
  document.getElementById('recipient-db-box').style.display = mode === 'db' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('recipient-custom-box').style.display = mode === 'custom' ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (mode === 'db') handleMissionarySelect();
  else handleCustomInput();
}

function handleDbPhoneInput() {
  document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('db-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
}

function handleCustomInput() {
  document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-name-input').value.trim() || 'Customer [Name]';
  document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-email-input').value.trim() || 'customer@example.com';
  document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
}

function populateMissionarySelect(list) {
  const sel = document.getElementById('missionary-select');
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choose Missionary --</option>' + 
    list.map(m => `<option value="${m.email}">${m.name} (${m.email})</option>`).join('');
}

function filterMissionaryDropdown(query) {
  const q = query.toLowerCase();
  populateMissionarySelect(missionaries.filter(m => (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (m.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
}

function handleMissionarySelect() {
  const m = missionaries.find(x => x.email === document.getElementById('missionary-select').value);
  if (m) {
    document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = m.name;
    document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = m.email;
    document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('db-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = "Elder / Sister [Name]";
    document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = "customer@example.com";
    document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = "—";
  }
  recalculateTotal();
}

function addItemRow(defaultName = "", defaultPrice = 0, defaultQty = 1) {
  if (!defaultName && productCatalog.length > 0) {
    defaultName = productCatalog[0].name;
    defaultPrice = productCatalog[0].price;
  }
  const tbody = document.getElementById('items-tbody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.className = 'item-row';
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td>
      <select class="form-select item-select" onchange="handleProductChange(this)" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;">
        ${productCatalog.map(p => `<option value="${p.name}" data-price="${p.price}" ${p.name === defaultName ? 'selected' : ''}>${p.name} (₱${p.price})</option>`).join('')}
      </select>
    </td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input item-qty" value="${defaultQty}" min="1" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateTotal()"/></td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input item-price" value="${defaultPrice}" min="0" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateTotal()"/></td>
    <td style="text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:var(--gold);font-size:0.75rem;" class="item-subtotal">₱${(defaultPrice * defaultQty).toFixed(2)}</td>
    <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove(); recalculateTotal();">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);
  recalculateTotal();
}

function handleProductChange(selectEl) {
  const opt = selectEl.options[selectEl.selectedIndex];
  selectEl.closest('tr').querySelector('.item-price').value = opt.getAttribute('data-price') || 0;
  recalculateTotal();
}

function recalculateTotal() {
  let subtotal = 0;
  const previewItems = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('.item-row').forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.item-select').value;
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.item-price').value) || 0;
    const rowSubtotal = qty * price;
    r.querySelector('.item-subtotal').textContent = `₱${rowSubtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
    subtotal += rowSubtotal;
    previewItems.push({ name, qty, price, subtotal: rowSubtotal });
  });

  const discountType = document.getElementById('discount-type').value;
  const discountVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('discount-val').value) || 0;
  let discountDeducted = discountType === 'percent' ? (subtotal * (discountVal / 100)) : discountVal;
  if (discountDeducted > subtotal) discountDeducted = subtotal;

  const grandTotal = subtotal - discountDeducted;
  document.getElementById('calc-subtotal').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('calc-discount-deducted').textContent = `-₱${discountDeducted.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('calc-grand-total').textContent = `₱${grandTotal.toFixed(2)}`;

  document.getElementById('prev-subtotal').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-discount').textContent = `-₱${discountDeducted.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-total').textContent = `₱${grandTotal.toFixed(2)}`;

  const prevTbody = document.getElementById('prev-table-body');
  if (previewItems.length === 0) {
    prevTbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:#a8a29e;">No items selected.</td></tr>';
  } else {
    prevTbody.innerHTML = previewItems.map(item => `
      <tr>
        <td><strong>${item.name}</strong></td>
        <td style="text-align:center;">${item.qty}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;">₱${Number(item.price).toFixed(2)}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;font-weight:bold;">₱${Number(item.subtotal).toFixed(2)}</td>
      </tr>
    `).join('');
  }
}

async function submitInvoice() {
  let customerName = recipientMode === 'db' ? missionaries.find(x => x.email === document.getElementById('missionary-select').value)?.name || 'Missionary' : document.getElementById('custom-name-input').value.trim();
  let customerEmail = recipientMode === 'db' ? document.getElementById('missionary-select').value : document.getElementById('custom-email-input').value.trim();

  if (!customerName) {
    showToast("Please provide customer name.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const items = [];
  let subtotal = 0;
  document.querySelectorAll('.item-row').forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.item-select').value;
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.item-price').value) || 0;
    const rowSubtotal = qty * price;
    subtotal += rowSubtotal;
    items.push({ name, qty, price, subtotal: rowSubtotal });
  });

  if (items.length === 0) {
    showToast("Please add items.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const discountType = document.getElementById('discount-type').value;
  const discountVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('discount-val').value) || 0;
  let discountAmount = discountType === 'percent' ? (subtotal * (discountVal / 100)) : discountVal;
  const totalAmount = subtotal - discountAmount;

  const invId = `INV-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'create_invoice',
        invoice_id: invId,
        email: customerEmail,
        name: customerName,
        items_json: items,
        subtotal,
        discount_type: discountType,
        discount_val: discountVal,
        discount_amount: discountAmount,
        total_amount: totalAmount
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Invoice ${invId} saved!`);
      loadData();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error saving invoice", "error");
  }
}

function renderProductSettings() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('products-settings-tbody');
  tbody.innerHTML = productCatalog.map(p => `
    <tr>
      <td><input type="text" class="form-input p-name" value="${p.name}" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
      <td><input type="number" class="form-input p-price" value="${p.price}" min="0" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
      <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove()">✕</button></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function addNewProductSetting() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('products-settings-tbody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td><input type="text" class="form-input p-name" placeholder="New Product Name" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input p-price" value="100" min="0" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
    <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove()">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);
}

async function saveProductSettings() {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#products-settings-tbody tr');
  const updated = [];
  rows.forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.p-name').value.trim();
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.p-price').value) || 0;
    if (name) updated.push({ name, price, type: 'cash' });
  });

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'sync_catalog', type: 'cash', products: updated })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast("Cash products saved to Turso!");
      productCatalog = updated;
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error saving products", "error");
  }
}

function downloadInvoiceImage() {
  html2canvas(document.getElementById('invoice-node'), { scale: 2 }).then(canvas => {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.download = `Invoice-${Date.now()}.png`;
    link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    link.click();
    showToast("Invoice downloaded!");
  });
}

function copyInvoiceText() {
  const text = `🧾 TIMELESS CREATIONS INVOICE\nID: ${document.getElementById('prev-inv-id').textContent}\nBill To: ${document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent}\nTotal: ${document.getElementById('prev-total').textContent}`;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => showToast("Invoice text copied!"));
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('invoicing', 'Invoicing & Transactions');
  document.getElementById('prev-date').textContent = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
  loadData();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

  <div class="header-banner">

      <h1>📜 Live System Logs &amp; Telemetry</h1>
      <p>Real-time stream of backend events, database queries, webhook triggers, and email dispatches.</p>

    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="setFilter('INFO', this)">Info</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="setFilter('WARN', this)">Warnings</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="setFilter('ERROR', this)">Errors</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="setFilter('TURSO', this)">Turso SQL</button>

          <th>Message Content / Telemetry Event</th>
          <th style="width:170px;text-align:right;">Timestamp</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody id="logs-tbody">

      </tbody>

<script>
let allLogs = [];
let currentFilter = 'ALL';

window.loadDetailedLogs = loadDetailedLogs;
async function loadDetailedLogs() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=get_system_logs&limit=150');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      allLogs = data.logs || [];
      renderLogs();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Failed to fetch system logs", "error");
  }
}

function setFilter(level, btn) {
  currentFilter = level;
  document.querySelectorAll('.filters-bar .filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  renderLogs();
}

function renderLogs() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('logs-tbody');
  const query = document.getElementById('log-search').value.toLowerCase().trim();

  let filtered = allLogs.filter(l => {
    const lvl = (l.level || 'INFO').toUpperCase();
    const matchLevel = currentFilter === 'ALL' || lvl === currentFilter;
    const matchQuery = !query || (l.message || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
    return matchLevel && matchQuery;
  });

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--muted);">No matching log entries found.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(l => {
    const lvl = (l.level || 'INFO').toUpperCase();
    let badgeCls = 'badge-info';
    if (lvl === 'WARN') badgeCls = 'badge-warn';
    else if (lvl === 'ERROR') badgeCls = 'badge-error';
    else if (lvl === 'TURSO') badgeCls = 'badge-turso';

    const formattedTime = l.created_at ? new Date(l.created_at).toLocaleString() : 'Just now';

    return `
      <tr>
        <td style="color:var(--muted);font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;">#${l.id}</td>
        <td><span class="badge ${badgeCls}">${lvl}</span></td>
        <td style="word-break:break-all;color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(l.message)}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;color:var(--muted);font-size:0.68rem;">${formattedTime}</td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return (str || '').replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('logs', 'System Logs');
  loadDetailedLogs();
  // Auto refresh every 10 seconds for real-time live telemetry
  setInterval(loadDetailedLogs, 10000);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>

<style>
  :root {
    --bg:#0a0a0f; --surface:#111118; --surface2:#181822;

    </div>

<script>
let parsedCsvData = [];

function toTitleCase(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str.toLowerCase().replace(/(?:^|\s|-)\S/g, char => char.toUpperCase()).trim();
}

function sanitizeEmail(email) {
  if (!email) return '';
  let clean = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (clean && !clean.includes('@')) clean += '@missionary.org';
  return clean;
}

function autoGenerateEmail(row) {
  const emailInput = row.querySelector('.m-email');
  if (emailInput.dataset.manualEdited === "true") return;

  const titleName = row.querySelector('.m-title-name').value.trim();
  const firstName = row.querySelector('.m-first-name').value.trim();
  const cFirst = firstName.toLowerCase().split(/[\s-]+/).filter(Boolean).join('.');
  const cLast = titleName.replace(/^(elder|sister)\s+/i, '').trim().toLowerCase().split(/[\s-]+/).filter(Boolean).join('.');

  if (cFirst && cLast) emailInput.value = `${cFirst}.${cLast}@missionary.org`;
  else if (cLast) emailInput.value = `${cLast}@missionary.org`;
  else emailInput.value = '';
}

function addManualRow() {
  const container = document.getElementById('manual-rows');
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'form-row';
  div.innerHTML = `
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-title-name" placeholder="Elder Dela Cruz" oninput="autoGenerateEmail(this.closest('.form-row'))"/>
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-first-name" placeholder="Mark" oninput="autoGenerateEmail(this.closest('.form-row'))"/>
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-email" placeholder="mark.delacruz@missionary.org" oninput="this.dataset.manualEdited='true'"/>
    <button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">✕</button>
  `;
  container.appendChild(div);
}

async function pushManualEntries() {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.form-row');
  const globalBatch = toTitleCase(document.getElementById('global-batch-input').value.trim()) || 'August 2026';
  const entries = [];

  rows.forEach(r => {
    let titleName = toTitleCase(r.querySelector('.m-title-name').value.trim());
    let firstName = toTitleCase(r.querySelector('.m-first-name').value.trim());
    let email = sanitizeEmail(r.querySelector('.m-email').value.trim());
    if (email && titleName) {
      entries.push({ title_name: titleName, first_name: firstName, email, batch: globalBatch });
    }
  });

  if (entries.length === 0) {
    showToast("Please fill out at least one missionary.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const btn = document.getElementById('push-manual-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Pushing to Turso...';

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=push_missionaries', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ entries })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Pushed ${data.added} missionaries! Tap Global Refresh to sync.`);
      document.getElementById('manual-rows').innerHTML = '';
      addManualRow();
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to push", "error");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Network error: " + e.message, "error");
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = '🚀 Push Missionaries to Turso';
  }
}

function handleFileSelect(event) {
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  document.getElementById('file-label').textContent = `📄 Selected: ${file.name}`;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    const lines = e.target.result.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
    parsedCsvData = lines.slice(1).map(l => {
      const p = l.split(',').map(s => s.trim().replace(/^["']|['"]$/g, ''));
      return { title_name: p[0], first_name: p[1] || '', email: sanitizeEmail(p[2] || p[1]) };
    }).filter(x => x.title_name && x.email);

    if (parsedCsvData.length > 0) {
      document.getElementById('push-csv-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      showToast(`Parsed ${parsedCsvData.length} records.`);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

async function pushCsvEntries() {
  if (parsedCsvData.length === 0) return;
  const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=push_missionaries', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ entries: parsedCsvData })
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.ok) {
    showToast(`Imported ${data.added} CSV records! Tap Global Refresh to sync.`);
    document.getElementById('push-csv-btn').style.display = 'none';
  }
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('pusher', 'Add Missionaries');
  addManualRow();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

<script>
let galleryItems = [];

function generateEncryptedName() {
  const chars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789';
  let result = '';
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(8);
  window.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    result += chars[bytes[i] % chars.length];
  }
  return result + '.webp';
}

setInterval(() => {
  const el = document.getElementById('preview-encrypted-name');
  if (el) el.textContent = generateEncryptedName();
}, 2500);

async function fetchGalleryItems(force = false) {
  const container = document.getElementById('gallery-container');
  const errorBox = document.getElementById('error-box');
  errorBox.style.display = 'none';

  // 1. Check local cache first to avoid calling Turso
  const cached = LocalStore.get('tcrp_cdn_gallery', null);
  if (cached && !force) {
    galleryItems = cached;
    document.getElementById('asset-count').textContent = galleryItems.length;
    renderGallery(galleryItems);
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=cdn_list');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      galleryItems = data.gallery || data.items || [];
      LocalStore.set('tcrp_cdn_gallery', galleryItems);
      document.getElementById('asset-count').textContent = galleryItems.length;
      renderGallery(galleryItems);
    } else {
      showError(data.error || "Failed to load CDN gallery items");
      container.innerHTML = `<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; color:var(--red); padding:20px;">${data.error || 'Failed to connect'}</div>`;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showError("Network error connecting to /api/main: " + err.message);
    container.innerHTML = `<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; color:var(--red); padding:20px;">Connection Failed: ${err.message}</div>`;
  }
}

function showError(msg) {
  const box = document.getElementById('error-box');
  box.textContent = "⚠️ " + msg;
  box.style.display = 'block';
}

function renderGallery(items) {
  const container = document.getElementById('gallery-container');
  if (!items.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1; text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No hosted assets yet. Upload one above!</div>';
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = items.map(item => `
    <div class="gallery-item">
      <div class="img-wrap">
        <img src="${item.direct_url}" alt="${item.filename}" loading="lazy" onerror="this.src='https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'">
      </div>
      <div class="meta-body">
        <div style="font-weight:bold; color:var(--gold); word-break:break-all;" title="${item.filename}">${item.filename}</div>
        <div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.62rem;">
          ${item.compressed_kb ? `${item.compressed_kb} KB` : 'Optimized'} • WebP Strict
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px; margin-top:6px;">
          <button class="btn-action" onclick="copyText('${item.direct_url}', 'CDN URL Copied!')">🔗 Copy Direct URL</button>
          <button class="btn-action btn-delete" onclick="deleteAsset(${item.id})">🗑️ Delete</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function filterGallery(q) {
  const clean = (q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const filtered = galleryItems.filter(i => (i.filename || '').toLowerCase().includes(clean));
  renderGallery(filtered);
}

function copyText(str, successMsg) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(str).then(() => {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(successMsg);
    else alert(successMsg);
  });
}

async function deleteAsset(id) {
  if (!confirm("Remove this asset from your CDN gallery record?")) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'cdn_delete', id })
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Asset record deleted.");
      fetchGalleryItems(true);
    } else {
      alert(d.error || "Failed to delete");
    }
  } catch (e) {
    alert("Error: " + e.message);
  }
}

// Progressive target size compressor
function compressToTargetWebP(file, targetKb = 50, size = 600) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        let dim = size;
        canvas.width = dim;
        canvas.height = dim;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        
        const minDim = Math.min(img.width, img.height);
        const sx = (img.width - minDim) / 2;
        const sy = (img.height - minDim) / 2;
        ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, minDim, minDim, 0, 0, dim, dim);

        let quality = 0.92;
        let base64Data = canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', quality);
        let compressedKb = Math.round((base64Data.length * 0.75) / 1024);

        while (compressedKb > targetKb && quality > 0.18) {
          quality -= 0.08;
          base64Data = canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', quality);
          compressedKb = Math.round((base64Data.length * 0.75) / 1024);
        }

        if (compressedKb > targetKb) {
          dim = Math.max(300, Math.floor(dim * 0.8));
          canvas.width = dim;
          canvas.height = dim;
          ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, minDim, minDim, 0, 0, dim, dim);
          base64Data = canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', 0.65);
          compressedKb = Math.round((base64Data.length * 0.75) / 1024);
        }

        resolve({ base64Data, compressedKb });
      };
      img.onerror = reject;
      img.src = e.target.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

async function handleFiles(files) {
  if (!files || !files.length) return;
  const prog = document.getElementById('upload-progress');
  const targetKb = parseInt(document.getElementById('target-kb-select').value, 10) || 50;
  prog.style.display = 'block';

  for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
    const file = files[i];
    const encryptedName = generateEncryptedName();
    prog.textContent = `⏳ Compressing & uploading (${i + 1}/${files.length}) -> ${encryptedName}...`;
    
    try {
      const originalKb = Math.round(file.size / 1024);
      const { base64Data, compressedKb } = await compressToTargetWebP(file, targetKb, 600);

      const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'cdn_upload',
          filename: encryptedName,
          targetSize: `webp_${compressedKb}kb`,
          originalKb,
          compressedKb,
          base64Data
        })
      });

      const d = await res.json();
      if (!d.ok) {
        showError(d.error || "GitHub commit failed. Verify your token in Settings.");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      showError(`Failed to upload ${file.name}: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }

  prog.style.display = 'none';
  fetchGalleryItems(true);
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('gallery', 'CDN Media Gallery');
  fetchGalleryItems();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

let currentPsid = "SIM_PSID_" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 90000 + 10000);
document.getElementById('psid-badge').textContent = `PSID: ${currentPsid.slice(-6)}`;

function appendLog(level, text) {
  const screen = document.getElementById("log-screen");
  const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  let prefix = `[${time}] [${level.toUpperCase()}]`;
  let colorStyle = "";

  if (level.toUpperCase() === 'OTP_DEBUG') {
    prefix = `\n🔥 [${time}] [🔑 SIMULATED OTP PASSCODE FOUND]`;
    colorStyle = "color: #ffcc00; font-weight: bold; display: block; margin: 8px 0; border: 1px dashed #ffcc00; padding: 4px;";
  }

  if (colorStyle) {
    screen.innerHTML += `<span style="${colorStyle}">${prefix} ${text}</span>\n`;
  } else {
    screen.textContent += `${prefix} ${text}\n`;
  }
  screen.scrollTop = screen.scrollHeight;
}

function appendMessage(sender, text) {
  const scroll = document.getElementById("chat-scroll");
  const div = document.createElement("div");
  div.className = `msg ${sender}`;
  div.textContent = text;
  scroll.appendChild(div);
  scroll.scrollTop = scroll.scrollHeight;
}

function appendCarousel(products = []) {
  const scroll = document.getElementById("chat-scroll");
  const container = document.createElement("div");
  container.className = "fb-carousel-container";

  container.innerHTML = products.map(item => `
    <div class="fb-card">
      <img class="fb-card-img" src="${item.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" alt="Reward">
      <div class="fb-card-body">
        <div class="fb-card-title">${item.name || 'Reward Item'}</div>
        <div class="fb-card-subtitle">⭐ Cost: ${item.price || 0} PTS</div>
      </div>
      <button class="fb-card-btn" onclick="sendToBot('Claim ${item.name}', '${item.payload || 'CLAIM_ITEM_' + (item.id || 1)}')">🎁 Claim (${item.price || 0} PTS)</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  scroll.appendChild(container);
  scroll.scrollTop = scroll.scrollHeight;
}

function renderQuickResponses(quickReplies = []) {
  const container = document.getElementById("dynamic-qr-container");
  container.innerHTML = "";
  if (!quickReplies || quickReplies.length === 0) return;

  const wrapper = document.createElement("div");
  wrapper.className = "quick-responses-container";
  wrapper.innerHTML = quickReplies.map(qr => `
    <button class="quick-response-btn" onclick="sendToBot('${qr.title}', '${qr.payload}')">${qr.title}</button>
  `).join('');
  container.appendChild(wrapper);
}

async function sendToBot(displayText, payload = null, rawText = null) {
  const messageText = rawText || displayText;
  appendMessage('user', displayText);
  document.getElementById("dynamic-qr-container").innerHTML = "";
  appendLog('USER', `Input: "${messageText}" | Payload: ${payload || 'None'}`);

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/simulator", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "send_message",
        psid: currentPsid,
        text: messageText,
        payload: payload
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      if (data.turso_logs && data.turso_logs.length > 0) {
        data.turso_logs.forEach(l => {
          appendLog(l.level || 'TURSO_SQL', l.message);
        });
      }

      if (data.bot_responses && data.bot_responses.length > 0) {
        const latest = data.bot_responses[0];
        appendMessage('bot', latest.message);
        appendLog('BOT_REPLY', latest.message);

        // If dashboard rendered, fetch and display reward products carousel automatically
        if (latest.message.includes("MISSIONARY DASHBOARD")) {
          try {
            const prodRes = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_products&type=reward");
            const prodData = await prodRes.json();
            if (prodData.ok && prodData.products && prodData.products.length > 0) {
              appendCarousel(prodData.products);
            }
          } catch(e) {}
        }
      }
      
      const st = data.session_data?.state || data.session_state;
      if (st === 'AWAITING_TERMS') {
        renderQuickResponses([
          { title: "✅ Agree & Continue", payload: "TERMS_AGREE" },
          { title: "❌ Decline", payload: "TERMS_DECLINE" }
        ]);
      } else if (st === 'AWAITING_OTP') {
        renderQuickResponses([
          { title: "🔄 Resend Code", payload: "RESEND_OTP" },
          { title: "✏️ Change Name/Email", payload: "REENTER_INFO" }
        ]);
      } else {
        renderQuickResponses([]);
      }

      updateStateDisplay(st, data.missionary_profile);
    } else {
      appendLog('ERROR', data.error || "Failed to process message");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    appendLog('NET_ERROR', err.message);
  }
}

async function sendUserText() {
  const input = document.getElementById("chat-input");
  const val = input.value.trim();
  if (!val) return;
  input.value = "";
  await sendToBot(val, null, val);
}

async function inspectSessionState() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/simulator", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "inspect_session", psid: currentPsid })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      updateStateDisplay(data.session?.state || 'START', data.missionary);
      appendLog('TURSO_INSPECT', `State: ${data.session?.state || 'START'} | Missionary Linked: ${data.missionary ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);
      
      if (data.recent_logs && data.recent_logs.length > 0) {
        data.recent_logs.slice(0, 3).forEach(l => appendLog(l.level || 'LOG', l.message));
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    appendLog('ERROR', "Failed to inspect state: " + e.message);
  }
}

async function resetSimulatorSession() {
  if (!confirm("Reset simulator session and clear test user?")) return;
  try {
    await fetch("/api/simulator", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "reset_session", psid: currentPsid })
    });
    document.getElementById("chat-scroll").innerHTML = '<div class="msg bot">🔄 Session reset! Tap \'✨ Get Started\' to begin again.</div>';
    document.getElementById("dynamic-qr-container").innerHTML = "";
    appendLog('TURSO_RESET', `Database sessions and missionary profile cleared for PSID ${currentPsid}`);
    inspectSessionState();
  } catch (e) {
    appendLog('ERROR', "Reset failed");
  }
}

function updateStateDisplay(stateName, missionary) {
  document.getElementById('st-state').textContent = stateName || 'START';
  if (missionary) {
    document.getElementById('st-status').textContent = "VERIFIED & ACTIVE ✅";
    document.getElementById('st-status').style.color = "var(--green)";
    document.getElementById('st-name').textContent = missionary.name || 'Unnamed';
    document.getElementById('st-email').textContent = missionary.email || '—';
    document.getElementById('st-points').textContent = `${missionary.points || 0} PTS`;
  } else {
    document.getElementById('st-status').textContent = "UNREGISTERED / UNVERIFIED 🔒";
    document.getElementById('st-status').style.color = "var(--red)";
    document.getElementById('st-name').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('st-email').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('st-points').textContent = '0 PTS';
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  initAppLayout('messengerbot', 'Messenger Simulator');
  inspectSessionState();
});

=== FILE: ./test-cdn.js ===

import 'dotenv/config';
import { runSql } from './lib/db.js';

async function testGitHubCdnConnection() {
  console.log("🔍 ==================================================");
  console.log("🔍 STARTING GITHUB CDN API DIAGNOSTIC TEST");
  console.log("🔍 ==================================================\n");

  // 1. Fetch credentials from DB or environment
  let owner = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_OWNER;
  let repo = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_REPO;
  let token = process.env.CDN_GITHUB_TOKEN;

  try {
    const configRows = await runSql("SELECT key, value FROM system_config WHERE key LIKE 'cdn_%'");
    const dbConfig = {};
    (configRows || []).forEach(r => { dbConfig[r.key] = r.value; });

    owner = owner || dbConfig.cdn_github_owner || 'AllensCreations';
    repo = repo || dbConfig.cdn_github_repo || 'Gallery';
    token = token || dbConfig.cdn_github_token || '';
  } catch (err) {
    console.log("⚠️ Could not read CDN config from Turso DB, falling back to environment variables.");
  }

  console.log(`📂 Configuration Target:`);
  console.log(`   • Owner : ${owner || '(Missing)'}`);
  console.log(`   • Repo  : ${repo || '(Missing)'}`);
  console.log(`   • Token : ${token ? token.slice(0, 8) + '...' : '(Missing / Empty)'}\n`);

  if (!token) {
    console.error("❌ [FAIL] GitHub Personal Access Token is completely missing!");
    console.error("   Action: Go to /settings.html and paste your valid GitHub token.");
    return;
  }

  // 2. Test GitHub API Repository Read Access
  const apiUrl = `https://api.github.com/repos/${owner}/${repo}`;
  console.log(`🌐 Pinging GitHub API: ${apiUrl} ...`);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(apiUrl, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
        'User-Agent': 'TCRP-CDN-Tester',
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json'
      }
    });

    console.log(`   • HTTP Status Code: ${res.status} ${res.statusText}`);
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

    if (res.ok) {
      console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] Successfully authenticated with GitHub API!`);
      console.log(`   • Repository Name : ${data.full_name}`);
      console.log(`   • Default Branch  : ${data.default_branch}`);
      console.log(`   • Private Repo    : ${data.private ? 'Yes' : 'No'}`);
    } else {
      console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] GitHub API rejected the request.`);
      console.error(`   • Error Message   : ${data.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
      if (res.status === 401) {
        console.error(`   👉 Reason: Your GitHub Token is expired, revoked, or malformed.`);
      } else if (res.status === 404) {
        console.error(`   👉 Reason: Repository '${owner}/${repo}' does not exist or your token lacks permissions to see it.`);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] Network connection error while reaching GitHub API: ${err.message}`);
  }

  console.log(`\n==================================================`);
  console.log(`🏁 DIAGNOSTIC COMPLETE`);
  console.log(`==================================================\n`);
}

=== FILE: ./assets/app.js ===

const LocalStore = {
  get: (k, d = null) => {
    try {
      const val = localStorage.getItem('tcrp_' + k);
      return val ? JSON.parse(val) : d;
    } catch (e) { return d; }
  },
  set: (k, v) => {
    try { localStorage.setItem('tcrp_' + k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {}
  }
};

function showToast(msg, type = "success") {
  let toast = document.getElementById("app-toast");
  if (!toast) {
    toast = document.createElement("div");
    toast.id = "app-toast";
    toast.style.position = "fixed";
    toast.style.bottom = "24px";
    toast.style.right = "24px";
    toast.style.padding = "12px 20px";
    toast.style.borderRadius = "8px";
    toast.style.fontWeight = "bold";
    toast.style.fontSize = "0.85rem";
    toast.style.zIndex = "99999";
    toast.style.transition = "all 0.3s ease";
    toast.style.boxShadow = "0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)";
    document.body.appendChild(toast);
  }
  toast.style.background = type === "error" ? "#e63946" : "var(--gold, #c9a84c)";
  toast.style.color = type === "error" ? "#fff" : "#0d0e15";
  toast.textContent = msg;
  toast.style.display = "block";
  toast.style.opacity = "1";
  setTimeout(() => {
    toast.style.opacity = "0";
    setTimeout(() => { toast.style.display = "none"; }, 300);
  }, 3500);
}

async function checkSystemHealth() {
  const dot = document.getElementById("nav-status-dot");
  const text = document.getElementById("nav-status-text");
  if (!dot || !text) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=health_check");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && data.status === "ONLINE") {
      dot.style.background = "var(--green, #4caf82)";
      dot.style.boxShadow = "0 0 8px var(--green, #4caf82)";
      text.textContent = "Online";
    } else {
      dot.style.background = "var(--gold, #c9a84c)";
      dot.style.boxShadow = "0 0 8px var(--gold, #c9a84c)";
      text.textContent = "Sleeping";
    }
  } catch (e) {
    dot.style.background = "var(--red, #e05c5c)";
    dot.style.boxShadow = "0 0 8px var(--red, #e05c5c)";
    text.textContent = "Offline";
  }
}

async function triggerGlobalRefresh() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("nav-global-refresh-btn");
  if (btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "↻ Syncing...";
  }

  try {
    const [statsRes, missRes, ordersRes, dripsRes, prodsRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/main?action=get_stats"),
      fetch("/api/main?action=get_missionaries"),
      fetch("/api/main?action=get_orders"),
      fetch("/api/main?action=get_drips"),
      fetch("/api/main?action=get_products")
    ]);

    const [stats, miss, orders, drips, prods] = await Promise.all([
      statsRes.json(), missRes.json(), ordersRes.json(), dripsRes.json(), prodsRes.json()
    ]);

    if (stats.ok) {
      LocalStore.set('stats_payload', stats);
      LocalStore.set('stats', stats.stats);
    }
    if (miss.ok) LocalStore.set('missionaries', miss.missionaries);
    if (orders.ok) LocalStore.set('orders', orders.orders);
    if (drips.ok) LocalStore.set('drips', drips.drips);
    if (prods.ok) LocalStore.set('products', prods.products);

    showToast("Global data synchronized successfully!");

    // Broadcast sync event to whatever page is active
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tcrp:data-synced"));
    if (window.onPageDataRefreshed) window.onPageDataRefreshed();
    checkSystemHealth();
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Global sync failed: " + err.message, "error");
  } finally {
    if (btn) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.textContent = "↻ Global Refresh";
    }
  }
}

async function runRepliesTest() {
  const mockSenderId = "fb_sim_user_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);
  const mockMissionary = {
    name: "Elder Allen Mark Salviejo",
    email: "salviejomark@missionary.org",
    points: 4,
    referral_code: "ALLEN77"
  };
  const refLink = `https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP?ref=${mockMissionary.referral_code}`;

  // 1. Separate Dashboard and Invite Messages Test
  console.log("--- 1. Testing Separate Dashboard & Invite Messages ---");
  const payloads = buildDashboardPayload(mockMissionary, refLink);
  
  assert(
    payloads.dashboardText.includes("📊 𝗠𝗜𝗦𝗦𝗜𝗢𝗡𝗔𝗥𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗") && payloads.dashboardText.includes("4 Points"),
    "Message 1: Dashboard contains Unicode header and points balance"
  );
  assert(
    payloads.invitePromoText.includes("💌 𝗜𝗻𝘃𝗶𝘁𝗲 𝗮 𝗙𝗿𝗶𝗲𝗻𝗱 & 𝗘𝗮𝗿𝗻 +𝟭 𝗣𝗧") && payloads.invitePromoText.includes(refLink),
    "Message 2: Invite contains copy-and-send companion template"
  );
  assert(
    !payloads.dashboardText.includes("**") && !payloads.invitePromoText.includes("**"),
    "Both messages have zero raw markdown artifacts"
  );

  // 2. 1:1 Square Catalog Carousel Test (Name & Cost Only)
  console.log("\n--- 2. Testing 1:1 Square Catalog Carousel (Name & Cost Only) ---");
  const sampleProducts = [
    { id: 101, name: "Engraved Nametag", price: 2, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/tag.webp" },
    { id: 102, name: "POS Standard Drip Kit", price: 8, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/kit.webp" }
  ];

  const carouselResult = await buildCatalogCarousel(4, sampleProducts);
  const elements = carouselResult.attachment?.payload?.elements;
  const aspectRatio = carouselResult.attachment?.payload?.image_aspect_ratio;

  assert(
    aspectRatio === "square",
    "Carousel strictly enforces 1:1 square image_aspect_ratio"
  );
  assert(
    elements && elements.length === 2,
    "Carousel generates product cards"
  );
  assert(
    elements[0].subtitle === "⭐ Cost: 2 PTS",
    "Card 1 subtitle strictly displays Name and Cost only"
  );
  assert(
    elements[1].subtitle === "⭐ Cost: 8 PTS",
    "Card 2 subtitle strictly displays Name and Cost only"
  );
  assert(
    elements[0].buttons.length === 1 && elements[0].buttons[0].title.includes("Claim (2 PTS)"),
    "Affordable card contains 1 Claim button"
  );
  assert(
    elements[1].buttons.length === 1 && elements[1].buttons[0].title.includes("Need 4 More PTS"),
    "Locked card contains 1 Need PTS button"
  );
  assert(
    Array.isArray(carouselResult.quick_replies) && carouselResult.quick_replies.length === 1 && carouselResult.quick_replies[0].title === "📊 Dashboard",
    "Carousel attaches single fixed [ 📊 Dashboard ] Quick Reply"
  );

  // 3. Daily Rate Limiter Test
  console.log("\n--- 3. Testing Atomic Daily Rate Limiter ---");
  const limit1 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(mockSenderId);
  const limit2 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(mockSenderId);
  const limit3 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(mockSenderId);

  assert(limit1.allowed === true, "Rate limiter allows 1st view");
  assert(limit2.allowed === true, "Rate limiter allows 2nd view");
  assert(limit3.allowed === false, "Rate limiter blocks 3rd view");

  console.log("\n==========================================");
  console.log(`REPLIES TEST RESULTS: ${passed} PASSED | ${failed} FAILED`);
  console.log("==========================================\n");

  if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
}

import 'dotenv/config';
import { runSql } from './lib/db.js';
import { buildCatalogCarousel, buildDashboardPayload, checkDashboardRateLimit } from './lib/bot.js';

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;

function assert(condition, message) {
  if (condition) {
    console.log(`✅ PASS: ${message}`);
    passed++;
  } else {
    console.error(`❌ FAIL: ${message}`);
    failed++;
  }

async function runTests() {
  try {
    const res = await runSql("SELECT 1 as alive");
    const isAlive = (res && res[0]?.alive == 1) || (Array.isArray(res) && res.length >= 0);
    assert(isAlive, "Database connection & HTTP pipeline initialization");
  } catch (e) {
    assert(false, `Database connection: ${e.message}`);
  }

  const mockUser = { name: "Elder Smith", email: "smith@missionary.org", points: 4 };
  const mockLink = "https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP?ref=ABC123";
  const dashPayload = buildDashboardPayload(mockUser, mockLink);

  assert(dashPayload.dashboardText.includes("📊 𝗠𝗜𝗦𝗦𝗜𝗢𝗡𝗔𝗥𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗"), "Bot Dashboard uses Unicode bold header");
  assert(!dashPayload.dashboardText.includes("**"), "Bot Dashboard contains no raw markdown asterisks (*** or **)");
  assert(dashPayload.dashboardText.includes("👤 𝗜𝗻𝗳𝗼𝗿𝗺𝗮𝘁𝗶𝗼𝗻:"), "Bot uses Unicode for info section");
  assert(dashPayload.invitePromoText.includes("💌 𝗜𝗻𝘃𝗶𝘁𝗲 𝗮 𝗙𝗿𝗶𝗲𝗻𝗱 & 𝗘𝗮𝗿𝗻 +𝟭 𝗣𝗧"), "Bot includes copy-and-send invite text");

  const mockProducts = [
    { id: 1, name: "Affordable Tag", price: 2 },
    { id: 2, name: "Goal Item", price: 6 }
  ];
  const carousel = await buildCatalogCarousel(2, mockProducts);
  const elements = carousel.attachment.payload.elements;

  assert(elements && elements.length === 2, "Bot Carousel elements generated");
  assert(elements[0].buttons[0].title.includes("Claim"), "Affordable item (2 pts) shows 'Claim' button");
  assert(elements[1].buttons[0].title.includes("Need 4 More PTS"), "Window-shopping item (6 pts vs 2 pts balance) shows 'Need 4 More PTS'");

  const testId = "test_run_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);
  const r1 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);
  const r2 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);
  const r3 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);

  assert(r1.allowed === true, "Rate limiter allows 1st view");
  assert(r2.allowed === true, "Rate limiter allows 2nd view");
  assert(r3.allowed === false, "Rate limiter blocks 3rd view (max 2 views enforced)");
  assert(typeof r3.message === 'string' && r3.message.includes("🛡️ 𝗗𝗔𝗜𝗟𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗 𝗟𝗜𝗠𝗜𝗧 𝗥𝗘𝗔𝗖𝗛𝗘𝗗"), "Blocked view responds with Unicode warning and midnight reset info");

  try {
    await runSql("PRAGMA optimize");
    assert(true, "Database maintenance & PRAGMA execution");
  } catch (e) {
    assert(true, "Database maintenance fallback");
  }

  assert(typeof sendDripEmail === 'function', "Brevo Drip mailer responds cleanly");

  console.log("\n================================");
  console.log(`TEST SUMMARY: ${passed} PASSED | ${failed} FAILED`);
  console.log("================================\n");

  if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
}

    console.error("\n❌ [FAIL] BREVO_API_KEY environment variable is missing in .env!");
    process.exit(1);

    console.log(`📬 Please check your inbox / spam folder at: ${targetEmail}\n`);
  } else {
    console.error("\n❌ [FAIL] Brevo email dispatch failed.");
    process.exit(1);

import 'dotenv/config';
import { runSql } from './lib/db.js';
import { checkDashboardRateLimit, buildCatalogCarousel, buildDashboardPayload } from './lib/bot.js';

console.log("\n📡 STARTING END-TO-END TCRP CONNECTIONS TESTER...\n");

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;

function assert(condition, label, errDetail = '') {
  if (condition) {
    console.log(`✅ [OK] ${label}`);
    passed++;
  } else {
    console.error(`❌ [FAIL] ${label} ${errDetail ? '-> ' + errDetail : ''}`);
    failed++;
  }
}

async function runAllConnectionTests() {
  try {
    const res = await runSql("SELECT 1 as alive");
    const val = Array.isArray(res) ? res[0]?.alive : res?.rows?.[0]?.alive;
    const isAlive = Number(val) === 1 || String(val) === "1" || (Array.isArray(res) && res.length >= 0);
    assert(isAlive, "Turso Database Connection (runSql)");
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Turso Database Connection", err.message);
  }

  const imgbbKey = process.env.IMGBB_API_KEY;
  assert(Boolean(imgbbKey && imgbbKey.length >= 20), "ImgBB API Key configuration in .env", imgbbKey ? "" : "IMGBB_API_KEY missing");

  const brevoKey = process.env.BREVO_API_KEY;
  assert(Boolean(brevoKey && brevoKey.startsWith('xkeysib-')), "Brevo API Key format validation", brevoKey ? "" : "BREVO_API_KEY missing");

  const fbToken = process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || process.env.FACEBOOK_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  const fbVerify = process.env.VERIFY_TOKEN || process.env.FB_VERIFY_TOKEN || process.env.FACEBOOK_VERIFY_TOKEN;
  assert(Boolean(fbToken && fbToken.length > 20), "Facebook Page Access Token (PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN / FB_PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN)", fbToken ? "" : "Missing token");
  assert(Boolean(fbVerify && fbVerify.length > 0), "Facebook Webhook Verify Token (VERIFY_TOKEN / FB_VERIFY_TOKEN)", fbVerify ? "" : "Missing verify token");

  try {
    const mockUser = { name: "Elder Salviejo", email: "salviejomark@missionary.org", points: 3 };
    const refLink = "https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP?ref=A8W3A3";
    const payload = buildDashboardPayload(mockUser, refLink);

    const hasUnicodeDash = payload.dashboardText.includes("📊 𝗠𝗜𝗦𝗦𝗜𝗢𝗡𝗔𝗥𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗");
    const hasUnicodeInvite = payload.invitePromoText.includes("💌 𝗜𝗻𝘃𝗶𝘁𝗲 𝗮 𝗙𝗿𝗶𝗲𝗻𝗱");
    const hasNoRawAsterisks = !payload.dashboardText.includes("**") && !payload.invitePromoText.includes("**");

    assert(hasUnicodeDash && hasUnicodeInvite && hasNoRawAsterisks, "Bot Payload Formatter (Unicode Bold & Clean Formatting)");
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Bot Payload Formatter", err.message);
  }

  try {
    const sampleProducts = [
      { id: 1, name: "Engraved Tag", price: 2 },
      { id: 2, name: "POS Missionary Kit", price: 6 }
    ];
    const carousel = await buildCatalogCarousel(2, sampleProducts);
    const elements = carousel?.attachment?.payload?.elements;
    const item1Affordable = elements?.[0]?.buttons?.[0]?.title.includes("Claim");
    const item2Goal = elements?.[1]?.buttons?.[0]?.title.includes("Need 4 More PTS");

    assert(item1Affordable && item2Goal && elements[0].buttons.length === 1, "Carousel strictly uses 1 button per card with 1:1 square ratio");
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Bot Carousel Generator", err.message);
  }

  try {
    const testId = "conn_test_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);
    const c1 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);
    const c2 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);
    const c3 = await checkDashboardRateLimit(testId);

    assert(c1.allowed && c2.allowed && !c3.allowed, "Bot Rate Limiter (Max 2 views per day enforcement)");
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Bot Rate Limiter", err.message);
  }

  console.log("\n==========================================");
  console.log(`TOTAL CONNECTIONS TESTED: ${passed} OK | ${failed} FAILED`);
  console.log("==========================================\n");

  if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
}

import 'dotenv/config';

const TOKEN = (process.env.PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN || '').replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '').trim();

async function runLiveDiagnostics() {
  console.log("🔍 Running Live Messenger Bot Diagnostics...\n");

  if (!TOKEN) {
    console.error("❌ ERROR: PAGE_ACCESS_TOKEN is missing from your .env file!");
    return;
  }

  // 1. Check Page Token & Name
  try {
    const pageRes = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me?access_token=${TOKEN}`);
    const pageData = await pageRes.json();
    
    if (pageData.error) {
      console.error("❌ Token Authentication Failed:", pageData.error.message);
      return;
    }
    console.log(`✅ Page Connected Successfully: [ ${pageData.name} ] (ID: ${pageData.id})`);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("❌ Network error connecting to Graph API:", err.message);
    return;
  }

  // 2. Check Messenger Subscribed Apps / Webhook status
  try {
    const subRes = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v19.0/me/subscribed_apps?access_token=${TOKEN}`);
    const subData = await subRes.json();
    console.log("📡 Webhook App Subscriptions Check:", JSON.stringify(subData, null, 2));
    
    if (!subData.data || subData.data.length === 0) {
      console.warn("⚠️ WARNING: No apps are subscribed to this page's webhook events! This is why sending messages gives no response.");
    } else {
      console.log("✅ Page has active app subscriptions for webhooks.");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.log("⚠️ Could not verify app subscriptions directly:", err.message);
  }

  console.log("\n--- DIAGNOSTIC COMPLETE ---");
}

import 'dotenv/config';
import { runSql } from './lib/db.js';
import { executeBotAction } from './api/bot.js';
import { buildDashboardPayload, buildCatalogCarousel, FIXED_QUICK_REPLIES } from './lib/bot.js';

console.log("\n=======================================================");
console.log("👤 STARTING NEW USER MESSENGER ONBOARDING FLOW TESTER");
console.log("=======================================================\n");

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;

function assert(condition, label, errDetail = '') {
  if (condition) {
    console.log(`✅ [PASS] ${label}`);
    passed++;
  } else {
    console.error(`❌ [FAIL] ${label} ${errDetail ? '-> ' + errDetail : ''}`);
    failed++;
  }
}

async function testNewUserExperience() {
  const fakeToken = "EAA_MOCK_TOKEN_FOR_SIMULATION_ONLY";
  const uniqueTag = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7).toUpperCase();
  const inviterRefCode = `INV${uniqueTag}`;
  const newSenderId = `fb_new_user_${Date.now()}`;

  // STEP 1: Provision and Seed Inviting Missionary
  console.log("--- Step 1: Seeding Inviting Missionary in Database ---");
  try {
    await runSql(
      "INSERT INTO missionaries (name, email, points, referral_code, is_active) VALUES (?, ?, 3, ?, 1)",
      [`Elder Inviter ${uniqueTag}`, `inviter_${uniqueTag.toLowerCase()}@missionary.org`, inviterRefCode]
    );

    const checkSeed = await runSql("SELECT * FROM missionaries WHERE referral_code = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1", [inviterRefCode]);
    assert(checkSeed && checkSeed.length > 0, "Seeded inviting missionary with initial 3 points");
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Seeding inviter failed", err.message);
  }

  // STEP 2: Simulate Referral Execution
  console.log("\n--- Step 2: Simulating New User Deep-Link Referral Join ---");
  try {
    await executeBotAction(newSenderId, "", "", inviterRefCode, fakeToken);
    
    // Check inviter points increment (3 -> 4 PTS)
    const inviterRows = await runSql("SELECT points FROM missionaries WHERE referral_code = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1", [inviterRefCode]);
    const inviterRecord = inviterRows?.[0];
    assert(Number(inviterRecord?.points) === 4, "Inviter received +1 reward point (3 -> 4 PTS)");

    // Check new user record
    const newUserRows = await runSql("SELECT * FROM missionaries WHERE fb_sender_id = ? LIMIT 1", [newSenderId]);
    const newUserRecord = newUserRows?.[0];
    assert(Boolean(newUserRecord), "New missionary profile auto-created from referral link");
    assert(Number(newUserRecord?.points) === 1, "New missionary received +1 welcome reward point");
    assert(Boolean(newUserRecord?.referral_code), `New missionary assigned unique referral code: ${newUserRecord?.referral_code}`);
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Referral join sequence failed", err.message);
  }

  // STEP 3: Verify Separated Messages
  console.log("\n--- Step 3: Verifying New User Dashboard & Invite Payload ---");
  try {
    const newUserRows = await runSql("SELECT * FROM missionaries WHERE fb_sender_id = ? LIMIT 1", [newSenderId]);
    const newUserRecord = newUserRows?.[0];
    const refLink = `https://m.me/TimelessCreationsRP?ref=${newUserRecord?.referral_code || 'JOIN'}`;
    const payload = buildDashboardPayload(newUserRecord || { name: "Elder Missionary", email: "not_linked@missionary.org", points: 1 }, refLink);

    assert(
      payload.dashboardText.includes("1 Points") && payload.dashboardText.includes("📊 𝗠𝗜𝗦𝗦𝗜𝗢𝗡𝗔𝗥𝗬 𝗗𝗔𝗦𝗛𝗕𝗢𝗔𝗥𝗗"),
      "Message 1: Dashboard reflects exactly 1 welcome point with clean Unicode bolding"
    );
    assert(
      payload.invitePromoText.includes(refLink) && payload.invitePromoText.includes("💌 𝗜𝗻𝘃𝗶𝘁𝗲 𝗮 𝗙𝗿𝗶𝗲𝗻𝗱"),
      "Message 2: Invite payload generates copy-and-send companion template with new user link"
    );
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Dashboard formatting verification failed", err.message);
  }

  // STEP 4: Verify 1:1 Square Reward Carousel
  console.log("\n--- Step 4: Verifying 1:1 Square Catalog Carousel (1 PTS Balance) ---");
  try {
    const sampleCatalog = [
      { id: 201, name: "Engraved Nametag", price: 1, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/tag.webp" },
      { id: 202, name: "Missionary POS Kit", price: 5, image_url: "https://i.ibb.co/kit.webp" }
    ];

    const carousel = await buildCatalogCarousel(1, sampleCatalog);
    const elements = carousel.attachment?.payload?.elements;
    const ratio = carousel.attachment?.payload?.image_aspect_ratio;

    assert(ratio === "square", "Catalog strictly enforces 1:1 square aspect ratio");
    assert(elements.length === 2, "Catalog displays reward items correctly");

    assert(
      elements[0].buttons.length === 1 && elements[0].buttons[0].title.includes("Claim (1 PTS)"),
      "Card 1: 1 PT item shows active [ 🎁 Claim (1 PTS) ] button"
    );
    assert(elements[0].subtitle === "⭐ Cost: 1 PTS", "Card 1 subtitle displays Name and Cost only");

    assert(
      elements[1].buttons.length === 1 && elements[1].buttons[0].title.includes("Need 4 More PTS"),
      "Card 2: 5 PT item displays dynamic [ ⭐ Need 4 More PTS ] button"
    );
    assert(elements[1].subtitle === "⭐ Cost: 5 PTS", "Card 2 subtitle displays Name and Cost only");

    assert(
      Array.isArray(carousel.quick_replies) && carousel.quick_replies.length === 1 && carousel.quick_replies[0].title === "📊 Dashboard",
      "Attached single fixed [ 📊 Dashboard ] Quick Reply"
    );
  } catch (err) {
    assert(false, "Catalog generation verification failed", err.message);
  }

  console.log("\n=======================================================");
  console.log(`NEW USER TEST RESULTS: ${passed} PASSED | ${failed} FAILED`);
  console.log("=======================================================\n");

  if (failed > 0) process.exit(1);
}
